{"parentUuid":"449ed9f4-f839-4a17-a8ac-d84693137852","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"ff672ad9-cc7f-4934-8af5-a1eaa250ea13","agentId":"a7b9f01d8dbd03b21","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01CzrjmypNnuRP2cxgZjWR9P","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t---\n2\tid: trace-map-node-controls\n3\ttitle: \"Trace Map Node Controls — inspector logging panel + long-polling off-by-default with temporary capture\"\n4\tepic: enhanced-logging\n5\tstatus: ready-for-dev\n6\tsecurity_review: required\n7\tmatrix: false\n8\tui: true\n9\tcreated: 2026-07-07\n10\t---\n11\t\n12\t# Trace Map Node Controls — Per-Node Logging Inspector & Temporary Capture\n13\t\n14\t## Story\n15\t\n16\t**As** a Nebu platform operator managing production logs,\n17\t**I want** the Trace Map inspector to become a logging control panel where I can configure per-endpoint collection levels and retention, and enable temporary logging on long-polling endpoints that are off by default,\n18\t**so that** I can fine-tune collection policy without leaving the Trace Map view, safely experiment with high-frequency endpoints via time-limited capture, and keep the map signal-to-noise ratio high by defaulting polling endpoints to off.\n19\t\n20\tThis story completes the approved Leitstand design (spec §4 and §5 from `_bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/design/trace-map-leitstand-spec.md`), building on the already-shipped `trace-map-leitstand` story. The inspector panel, previously read-only with live stats only, becomes an interactive logging control center. Long-polling endpoints (config-driven list, default: `/_matrix/client/v3/sync`, `/admin/keepalive`, `/health`) are rendered off by default — gray, dashed, count \"off\", no collection — and a single click enables TEMPORARY capture for 5 minutes with a server-side timer, countdown display, auto-revert, and aria-live announcements.\n21\t\n22\t**This story SUPERSEDES the UI concept of `noise-endpoint-bucketing.md`** (status: ready-for-dev, with a \"Design Update — REALIGNMENT REQUIRED\" note): the bucketing-UI (collapse/expand category) is obsolete; the backend concept (config-driven low-signal endpoint list, off-by-default collection) is reused. See AC13 for the coordination note.\n23\t\n24\t### Design Source\n25\t\n26\tAuthoritative design: `_bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/design/trace-map-leitstand-spec.md` (Phil approved 2026-07-07).\n27\t- §4: Inspector control panel (logging controls)\n28\t- §5: Long-polling endpoints — off by default, temporary capture with countdown\n29\t- §8: Hard implementation constraints (cluster-safe timers, no node-local Process timers)\n30\t\n31\tVisual/interaction reference: `trace-map-leitstand-dummy.html` (interactive browser dummy), sections \"Inspector\" and gray-dashed off-nodes with countdown.\n32\t\n33\t**Out of scope:** The live-stream panel (already shipped in `trace-map-leitstand`); other monitoring features.\n34\t\n35\t## Acceptance Criteria\n36\t\n37\t### AC1: Inspector Logging Controls — Min Level Select\n38\t\n39\tWhen a node is inspected (focused/clicked on the Trace Map), the inspector panel below the live stats renders a \"Logging\" section with a **Min level select** (DEBUG/INFO/WARN/ERROR):\n40\t- The select is wired to the per-endpoint collection level from `admin-logs-retention-settings` (already done)\n41\t- On mount/focus, the select is pre-populated with the endpoint's current configured level from the server_config\n42\t- Changing the select triggers a server round-trip to persist the new level (via the existing `Nebu.Admin.DB.upsert_server_config/1` API)\n43\t- **For domain/nebu nodes**: the label reads \"Applies to all endpoints in this domain\" (or \"on the map\" for nebu); the setting cascades to all descendant endpoints as a batch operation\n44\t- **For satellite/endpoint nodes**: the label reads \"Applies to this endpoint only\"\n45\t- The select never loses focus or value during re-renders (pinned by a test: the panel is built once per inspected node, only stat values update per tick)\n46\t\n47\t### AC2: Inspector Logging Controls — Retention Select\n48\t\n49\tWhen a node is inspected, the \"Logging\" section also renders a **Retention select** (1 h / 24 h / 7 d / 30 d):\n50\t- Wired to the per-endpoint retention days from `admin-logs-retention-settings`\n51\t- Pre-populated from server_config on mount/focus\n52\t- Changing the select persists the new retention window\n53\t- Same cascade rules as AC1 (domain/nebu cascade; endpoint is solo)\n54\t- Same focus-preservation guarantee\n55\t\n56\t### AC3: Inspector Built Once Per Node, Only Stats Update\n57\t\n58\tThe inspector panel HTML structure and form elements are BUILT once when a node is inspected (focused/clicked). On every subsequent stat update (new log entry, window slide), only the **stat values** (request count, warning count, error count, last 5 events) are patched via LiveView assigns — not the entire panel markup.\n59\t\n60\t**Test pinned:** Click node → panel renders → assert select `value` and `focus` are correct. Inject log entry → stat patch happens → assert select still has same `value` and is still focused (if it was before).\n61\t\n62\t### AC4: Long-Polling Off-by-Default — Config-Driven List\n63\t\n64\tA config-driven list of long-polling endpoints (default: `/_matrix/client/v3/sync`, `/admin/keepalive`, `/health`) are rendered as **logging-off nodes**:\n65\t- **Rendered state**: gray fill (base-200 @ 15%), text-disabled dashed 1.5px stroke, count shows \"off\" text (not a number), no status color, no error/warn badges\n66\t- **Data not collected**: entries for these endpoints are NOT persisted to `operational_logs` (server-side enforcement via policy), and the status window shows zero requests/warnings/errors\n67\t- **No status propagation**: a parent domain with ALL children as off-endpoints shows no status (no error/warn/ok; just gray and dashed like its children)\n68\t- **Backend concept reused from noise-endpoint-bucketing**: a hardcoded constant (or config key) defines which endpoints are in the off-by-default list; the same list is used by all consuming stories (reuse potential for future war-room-dedicated-page)\n69\t- **Configuration**: the list is defined as `@long_polling_endpoints = [...]` (or similar) in `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap`, with clear documentation; a public function `def long_polling_endpoints()` exposes it for reuse\n70\t\n71\t### AC5: Click Off-Node Enables Temporary Capture (5 Minutes)\n72\t\n73\tClicking on an off-tier endpoint enables **temporary capture** for 5 minutes:\n74\t- Server-side timer: a stateful capture record is inserted into a temporary store (e.g., `admin_temporary_captures` table, or a volatile in-memory structure backed by a periodic cleanup task), recording `endpoint`, `enabled_at`, `expires_at` (now + 5 minutes), and `started_by` (current admin user)\n75\t- **NOT client-only state**: the capture timer is enforced server-side; no client-side `setTimeout` that breaks on page reload/crash\n76\t- **Cluster-safe**: if the deployment is multi-node, the temporary capture record is in the shared database or a distributed cache (follow the pattern used by `war_room_manager` — design decision notes below)\n77\t- While capturing, the node becomes **live** with dashed status stroke + countdown on the node and in the inspector panel\n78\t- Visual state: \"temp capture\" from the spec (as status, dashed stroke, countdown mm:ss)\n79\t- Log entries for the endpoint are now collected (the policy check skips the off-list while capture is active)\n80\t- **Early disable button**: the inspector panel shows \"Disable now\" button (when capture is active), which manually ends the capture before expiry\n81\t- **Auto-revert**: when the timer expires, the endpoint reverts to off automatically with no manual intervention\n82\t- **Aria-live announcements**: enable event announces \"Capture enabled for X endpoint — 5 minutes\" (or similar); disable/expiry announce \"Capture ended\" or \"5 minutes expired\"\n83\t\n84\t### AC6: Temporary Capture Countdown Display\n85\t\n86\tWhen capture is active on a node:\n87\t- The node itself displays countdown mm:ss (e.g., \"04:32\" for 4 minutes 32 seconds remaining)\n88\t- The inspector panel displays the same countdown + \"Disable now\" button\n89\t- Countdown updates live every second (client-side, computed from server's `expires_at` — no server tick needed; the expiry time is set once, client updates every 1 s via JS hook)\n90\t- On expiry, the countdown disappears and the node reverts to off state (gray + dashed, count \"off\", no countdown)\n91\t\n92\t### AC7: While Capturing, Stream Filter Works Normally\n93\t\n94\tWhile a node is in temporary-capture state:\n95\t- A second click (or Enter/Space key) toggles the node's inclusion in the stream filter (same as any other node)\n96\t- The capture timer continues regardless of filter state\n97\t- Clicking \"Disable now\" in the inspector ends the capture (node reverts to off, filter is NOT automatically removed if active)\n98\t- This allows the operator to enable capture, then immediately filter by that endpoint to watch its logs, then disable capture early if they see what they need\n99\t\n100\t### AC8: Visual States — Off (Logging-Off) and Temp (Temporary Capture)\n101\t\n102\tTwo new visual states, defined in the Trace Map legend (already rendered in `trace-map-leitstand`):\n103\t- **Off**: base-200 @ 15% fill, text-disabled dashed 1.5px stroke, \"off\" text label, no status color\n104\t- **Temp**: status color (ok/warn/error) fill, status color dashed stroke, countdown mm:ss on the node, no badges (until capture ends and status is evaluated)\n105\t\n106\tStatus is never color-alone; the \"off\" text, countdown, and aria-labels make the state explicit.\n107\t\n108\t### AC9: Accessibility — Labels, Keyboard, Announcements\n109\t\n110\t- Inspector selects (min level, retention) are labelled with `<label for=\"...\">` elements\n111\t- Clicking an off-node, pressing Enter/Space on a focused off-node, or clicking \"Disable now\" dispatches aria-live announcements:\n112\t  - Enable: \"Temporary capture enabled for [endpoint name] · 5 minutes\"\n113\t  - Disable: \"Temporary capture disabled for [endpoint name]\"\n114\t  - Expiry: \"Temporary capture expired for [endpoint name]\"\n115\t- Aria-labels on off-nodes read \"off\" (e.g., \"sync endpoint · /_matrix/client/v3/sync · logging off\")\n116\t- Aria-labels on temp-capture nodes include countdown (e.g., \"sync endpoint · /_matrix/client/v3/sync · temporary capture · 4 minutes 32 seconds remaining\")\n117\t- \"Disable now\" button has `aria-label=\"Disable capture for sync endpoint\"` or similar\n118\t\n119\t### AC10: CSP & Security Compliance\n120\t\n121\t- No inline JS; countdown updating via app.js `TraceMapLeitstand` hook (existing hook from `trace-map-leitstand`, extended with capture-state awareness)\n122\t- Inspector form: POST to a new server action or a LiveView event (`handle_event(\"configure_logging\", ...)`)\n123\t- Capture enable/disable: POST/DELETE or `handle_event(\"enable_capture\", ...)` / `handle_event(\"disable_capture\", ...)` on the LiveView\n124\t- CSRF token included in any form\n125\t- No raw endpoint names exposed in URLs (filter state is already URL-safe per `trace-map-leitstand`'s filter-state design)\n126\t\n127\t### AC11: E2E Test Coverage — Inspector Controls & Capture\n128\t\n129\tNew BDD scenarios (in `e2e/features/admin/logs-trace-map.feature`, or a new file if preferred):\n130\t\n131\t**Scenario A: Inspector loads with endpoint's current logging config**\n132\t- Given I click an endpoint node on the Trace Map\n133\t- Then the inspector panel shows the endpoint's current min level and retention from the config\n134\t- When I change the min level to DEBUG\n135\t- Then a server round-trip persists the change\n136\t- And the inspector panel's level select still has focus (pinned: focus is not lost)\n137\t\n138\t**Scenario B: Cascade — changing domain node config applies to all satellites**\n139\t- Given I click a domain node (e.g., \"Matrix Client\")\n140\t- Then the inspector panel's level select shows \"Applies to all endpoints in this domain\"\n141\t- When I change the level to WARN\n142\t- Then all satellites under the domain inherit WARN level (verified via a second page load or a Playwright DB query)\n143\t- And a success message appears (\"Updated X endpoints\")\n144\t\n145\t**Scenario C: Off-endpoint enable capture**\n146\t- Given an off-endpoint (e.g., \"/health\") is rendered as gray + dashed with count \"off\"\n147\t- When I click the node\n148\t- Then the inspector panel shows \"Logging is off\" + \"Enable capture for 5 minutes\" button (or similar)\n149\t- When I click \"Enable capture\"\n150\t- Then the node becomes live with countdown mm:ss (e.g., \"04:59\")\n151\t- And aria-live announces \"Temporary capture enabled for /health · 5 minutes\"\n152\t- And the countdown updates every second (visual test: verify 04:59 → 04:58 after ~1 second)\n153\t\n154\t**Scenario D: Countdown expires and auto-reverts**\n155\t- Given capture is active on an off-endpoint with countdown visible\n156\t- When I wait for the countdown to reach 00:00 (or simulate via server-side time-travel if testable)\n157\t- Then the node reverts to off state (gray + dashed, count \"off\", no countdown)\n158\t- And aria-live announces \"Temporary capture expired for /health\"\n159\t\n160\t**Scenario E: Early disable**\n161\t- Given capture is active with countdown \"02:15\"\n162\t- When I click \"Disable now\" in the inspector panel\n163\t- Then the node immediately reverts to off\n164\t- And aria-live announces \"Temporary capture disabled for /health\"\n165\t\n166\t**Scenario F: While capturing, filter toggle works**\n167\t- Given capture is active on \"/sync\"\n168\t- When I click the node again (while capturing)\n169\t- Then the node shows a selection ring (filter is toggled on)\n170\t- When the countdown expires\n171\t- Then the node reverts to off (filter may remain active or be cleared, per design decision in Dev Notes)\n172\t- And the stream continues to show entries if the filter was active\n173\t\n174\t### AC12: Persistence & Audit — Capture Enable/Disable Actions\n175\t\n176\t**Design decision point (to be clarified in Dev Notes):**\n177\t\n178\t**Option A (No audit log entry):** Temporary capture enable/disable are not recorded in the compliance audit log. They are user-facing logging experiments, not configuration changes.\n179\t\n180\t**Option B (Admin actions in audit log):** Temporary capture enable/disable are recorded as admin actions in `compliance_audit_log` (similar to how `admin-logs-retention-settings` records setting changes), with the format: `action: \"enable_temporary_capture\" / \"disable_temporary_capture\"`, `resource_type: \"endpoint\"`, `resource_id: endpoint_name`, `admin_user: current_user`, `timestamp: now()`.\n181\t\n182\t**For this story**, adopt **Option A (no audit)** as the baseline unless the security review mandates otherwise. The rationale: temporary captures are transient experiments (5 min expiry), not persistent configuration changes, and they're already logged implicitly by the operational_logs themselves (entries that wouldn't normally be persisted are being persisted, which is visible in the logs store).\n183\t\n184\t### AC13: Superseding noise-endpoint-bucketing Story\n185\t\n186\tThe story `docs/stories/enhanced-logging/noise-endpoint-bucketing.md` (status: ready-for-dev) proposed a \"Polling/Keepalive\" bucket/collapse-expand UI in the Trace Map's endpoint dimension. Phil's Leitstand redesign supersedes that UI concept.\n187\t\n188\t**This story supersedes that story's implementation scope by:**\n189\t1. Marking `noise-endpoint-bucketing` as status: `superseded` (with a note: \"UI concept merged into `trace-map-node-controls` — reuse backend constant\")\n190\t2. Reusing the backend concept: the canonical constant `@long_polling_endpoints` (or `@noise_endpoints`, using the naming from the superseded story for continuity) is the same list the superseded story would have used\n191\t3. Documenting the deprecation in `noise-endpoint-bucketing.md`'s frontmatter and a note in this story's Dev Notes\n192\t\n193\t**The PM/coordinator should:**\n194\t- Mark `noise-endpoint-bucketing` as status `superseded` in the planning artifact\n195\t- No implementation work is needed for that story (it is merged into this one)\n196\t\n197\t## Dev Notes\n198\t\n199\t### Files to Create/Modify\n200\t\n201\t**Modify:**\n202\t- `server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map.ex` (add inspector logging controls, off-endpoint list, capture timer logic, event handlers)\n203\t- `server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_status.ex` (may need to handle off-endpoints differently if they should show zero in the status window)\n204\t- `server/priv/static/assets/app.js` (`TraceMapLeitstand` hook extended: countdown timer updates, \"Disable now\" click handler)\n205\t- `server/assets/css/app.css` (visual states for off/temp if not already covered by `trace-map-leitstand`)\n206\t- `server/test/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_leitstand_test.exs` (extend existing tests to cover new inspector controls and capture logic)\n207\t- `e2e/features/admin/logs-trace-map.feature` (add 6 new scenarios A–F above)\n208\t- `e2e/step-definitions/admin/logs-trace-map.steps.ts` (step implementations for capture scenarios)\n209\t\n210\t**Create (if not already in place):**\n211\t- Migration or seed script: ensure `admin_temporary_captures` table exists (if using a DB-backed timer; see design decision below). Schema: `endpoint TEXT`, `enabled_at TIMESTAMP`, `expires_at TIMESTAMP`, `started_by TEXT`, `primary_key: (endpoint, started_by)` (exactly one active capture per endpoint per user, or per endpoint globally depending on design choice)\n212\t\n213\t### Design Decisions — Cluster-Safe Temporary Capture Timer\n214\t\n215\t**Problem:** A naive `Process.send_after(self(), {:revert_to_off, endpoint}, 5 * 60 * 1000)` only works on a single node. In a multi-node deployment, when the process crashes or the node is restarted, the timer is lost.\n216\t\n217\t**Solution:** Follow `Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager`'s pattern:\n218\t1. **Insert a record into a temporary captures table** (`admin_temporary_captures` or similar):\n219\t   - `endpoint TEXT PRIMARY KEY` (only one active capture per endpoint)\n220\t   - `enabled_at TIMESTAMP` (server time when capture started)\n221\t   - `expires_at TIMESTAMP` (server time = now + 5 minutes)\n222\t   - `started_by TEXT` (admin user who enabled it)\n223\t2. **Server-side enforcement at ingest time:** When a log entry arrives for an off-list endpoint, check if a record exists in the temporary captures table with `expires_at > now()`. If yes, persist the entry; if no, skip it (off-tier enforcement).\n224\t3. **Cleanup on a timer:** A periodic task (e.g., `Nebu.Admin.TemporaryCapturesCleaner` or integrated into the existing `Nebu.Web.LogStore.Sweeper`) runs every 10–30 seconds and deletes expired records (`DELETE FROM admin_temporary_captures WHERE expires_at < now()`).\n225\t4. **LiveView listener:** The LiveView's `handle_info/2` also checks the table on every PubSub message and patches nodes' state if a capture has expired.\n226\t\n227\t**Outcome:** Cluster-safe, survives node restarts, deterministic (server time, not wall-clock).\n228\t\n229\t**Alternative:** Use Redis for the temporary captures table (if Redis is in the deployment). Same logic, just swap the table for a Redis key with TTL. Not recommended unless Redis is mandatory anyway.\n230\t\n231\t**For testing:** Make the 5-minute duration configurable via an Application env var (e.g., `Application.get_env(:nebu, :temporary_capture_duration_s, 300)`), so tests can use a much shorter duration (e.g., 1 second) without waiting 5 minutes. The same pattern is used by `war_room_manager` (AC8/AC10 in `war-room-mode` story).\n232\t\n233\t### Architecture Notes\n234\t\n235\t**Inspector Control Panel:**\n236\t- The panel is rendered in `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap.render(:trace_map)`, below the live stats section\n237\t- The two selects (`<select name=\"min_level\">`, `<select name=\"retention_days\">`) are part of the inspector panel component\n238\t- Each select's `name` attribute is encoded with the inspected node's key (e.g., `min_level[/sync]`, `retention_days[/_matrix/client]`) to distinguish which node's config is being changed\n239\t- Or simpler: use a single form per inspector panel rebuild, with a hidden field storing the inspected node's key, and the selects are always for that one node\n240\t- On change event (phx-change or phx-submit), the handler extracts the node key and the new value, and calls the same backend that `admin-logs-retention-settings` uses to persist: `Nebu.Admin.DB.upsert_server_config/1` with a map like `%{\"log_min_level_/sync\" => :debug, \"log_retention_days_/sync\" => 7}`\n241\t\n242\t**Off-Endpoint List & Capture Logic:**\n243\t- Define `@long_polling_endpoints = [...]` in `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap`\n244\t- A public function `def long_polling_endpoints()` returns it (for reuse by other modules)\n245\t- In the Trace Map's render, filter nodes to check `Enum.member?(@long_polling_endpoints, node.key)` to determine if the node is off-tier\n246\t- In `handle_event(\"enable_capture\", ...)` or similar, insert a record into `admin_temporary_captures` and broadcast a PubSub message to update the LiveView\n247\t- The countdown display is computed client-side in the `TraceMapLeitstand` hook: `Math.max(0, (expires_at_ms - Date.now()) / 1000)` every second\n248\t\n249\t**Cascade for Domain/Nebu Nodes:**\n250\t- If the inspected node is a domain or nebu, the select change triggers a batch operation: instead of a single `upsert_server_config`, call a helper (e.g., `apply_config_to_descendants(node_key, new_config)`) that updates all satellite endpoints under the domain (or all endpoints on the map, for nebu)\n251\t- Implementation: for each descendant, call `upsert_server_config` in a transaction or a loop\n252\t- Success flash: \"Updated X endpoints\" (where X is the count of affected satellites)\n253\t\n254\t**Focus Preservation on Inspector Panel:**\n255\t- The inspector panel is rendered as a separate component (or template section) that is only re-rendered when the inspected node changes (not on every stat update)\n256\t- Use Phoenix.LiveView's `assign/3` for stat updates only; stat updates should NOT trigger a re-render of the selects\n257\t- In Playwright tests, verify that `await page.locator('select[name=\"min_level\"]').focus()` persists across stat updates (LiveView patches the stat values without touching the select element's DOM)\n258\t\n259\t### Testing Strategy\n260\t\n261\t**Unit tests** (`logs_live/trace_map_leitstand_test.exs`):\n262\t- Mount with an off-tier endpoint present → verify gray + dashed rendering, count shows \"off\"\n263\t- Click off-tier endpoint → enable capture → verify node state changes to temp, countdown appears\n264\t- Wait for expiry (or simulate via time-warp in test) → verify node reverts to off\n265\t- Click \"Disable now\" → verify immediate revert\n266\t- Inspector selects: change level → verify server round-trip and persistence\n267\t- Domain node: change level → verify batch update of all satellites (query DB to confirm)\n268\t- Focus preservation: click node → select gets focus → inject log entry (handle_info) → verify select still focused\n269\t\n270\t**Integration tests** (same file):\n271\t- Full-stack: enable capture → check `admin_temporary_captures` table → verify entry exists\n272\t- Cleanup timer: insert expired record → run cleanup task → verify record deleted\n273\t- PubSub broadcast: enable capture on one page → another page listening → verify state updates without reload\n274\t\n275\t**E2E tests** (`logs-trace-map.feature`):\n276\t- Scenarios A–F above, using Playwright to interact with UI and await countdown updates\n277\t- No sleeps: use `await page.waitForTimeout(1000)` or similar for the 1-second countdown update, or use `await page.waitForFunction(...)` to wait until countdown text changes\n278\t\n279\t### Reuse Points for Future Stories\n280\t\n281\t1. **Canonical noise endpoint list:** `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.long_polling_endpoints()` is the single source of truth for \"which endpoints are low-signal\" — reused by any future story that needs to identify polling endpoints (e.g., war-room-dedicated-page's \"safe scope\" option, trace-map-hierarchy's domain-grouping logic)\n282\t2. **Temporary capture pattern:** The `admin_temporary_captures` table and cleanup task provide a reusable template for other time-limited operations (though temporary capture itself is specific to this story)\n283\t3. **Inspector panel pattern:** The read-once-update-stats approach (panel built once per node, only stat values patched) is a good pattern for any inspector-like panel that needs to preserve form state during live updates\n284\t\n285\t### Security Notes\n286\t\n287\t- **Sensitive data:** Off-tier endpoints are still endpoints (routes), not payloads. Rendering them as \"off\" is transparent (they appear in the legend, in the map, on the inspector label). No sensitive data leakage.\n288\t- **Capture enable/disable:** Requires admin auth (inherited from the LogsLive's `:admin` gate). No open API.\n289\t- **Temporary capture duration:** Hardcoded to 5 minutes, enforced server-side, with a configurable test override. Operators cannot arbitrarily extend or remove the timer.\n290\t- **Audit (Option A chosen):** No compliance log entries, but the operations themselves are opaque to the operator (they enable capture, entries appear in `operational_logs`, they can read them). If a compliance review later requires audit entries, Option B can be added in a follow-up story.\n291\t\n292\t## Known Challenges & Design Decisions\n293\t\n294\t1. **Off-tier status propagation:** A parent domain with ALL children as off-tier (e.g., `/admin` with only `/admin/keepalive` as its satellite) should itself be off-tier (gray + dashed). Implement this by checking in the status-propagation logic: if all children are off, parent is off; otherwise, parent inherits worst-status from capturing children.\n295\t\n296\t2. **Capture expiry visibility:** The expiry is server-side, but the countdown display is client-side. If the browser tab is closed and reopened during a capture, the page loads fresh; on mount, the LiveView checks the `admin_temporary_captures` table and re-renders the node in temp-capture state with an updated countdown. This requires the `expires_at` timestamp to be sent to the client on every mount (or on every stat update, if the page is already loaded).\n297\t\n298\t3. **Multiple endpoints, multiple captures:** If the operator enables capture on two off-tier endpoints simultaneously, two rows exist in `admin_temporary_captures`. Each countdown is independent. Cleanup runs periodically and deletes both when their respective expiry times are reached. No special ordering or batching needed.\n299\t\n300\t4. **Filter state during capture:** If the operator enables capture on `/sync`, then filters by `/sync`, then disables capture early, the filter remains active (nodes can be filtered and captured independently). The stream continues to show `/sync` entries because the filter is active. Disabling capture does NOT clear filters (even if the node becomes off-tier again, it stays in the filter state).\n301\t\n302\t## References & Cross-Story Coordination\n303\t\n304\t- **Depends on:** `trace-map-leitstand` (shipped, provides the base Leitstand page and inspector panel structure)\n305\t- **Depends on:** `admin-logs-retention-settings` (shipped, provides config storage and API for log level/retention)\n306\t- **Blocks:** None (standalone feature)\n307\t- **Supersedes:** `noise-endpoint-bucketing` (UI concept merged into this story; backend concept reused)\n308\t- **Coordinates with:** `war-room-dedicated-page` (potential future reuse of the `long_polling_endpoints` constant for war-room scopes)\n309\t- **Coordinates with:** `trace-map-hierarchy` (potential future integration of off-tier endpoints into the hierarchy tree)\n310\t\n311\t## Definition of Ready\n312\t\n313\tAll ACs are present and testable. The story is ready for development once:\n314\t1. Security review confirms the temporary capture timer design is acceptable (or recommends an alternative)\n315\t2. PM clarifies AC12 (audit log: Option A or Option B) if a preference exists\n316\t3. Coordinates with `noise-endpoint-bucketing` to mark it as superseded\n317\t\n318\t## Story Amendments\n319\t\n320\t(None at time of creation.)\n321\t\n322\t## Questions for Dev Agent — ANSWERED (coordinator/design owner, 2026-07-07)\n323\t\n324\t1. **Cluster-safe timer design: database-backed** (`expires_at` timestamp column; state is a row, expiry is a comparison against now() — no process timer owns correctness). Follow the war_room_manager precedent for the polling/refresh side. Rationale: Nebu is compliance-native and self-hosted with multi-node ambitions (cluster-safety convention); in-memory captures silently outliving or vanishing across deploys/nodes is exactly the class of bug this epic keeps meeting.\n325\t\n326\t2. **AC12 audit logging: Option B — YES, audit-log capture enable/disable.** Enabling collection on a previously-off endpoint is an admin-initiated change to logging policy (same category as retention-settings changes — follow that story's audit precedent). Kassandra reviews this story; Option B preempts the obvious finding.\n327\t\n328\t3. **Capture duration via `Application.get_env(:nebu, :temporary_capture_duration_s, 300)`: approved** (war_room_manager pattern). E2E may alternatively seed an about-to-expire capture row via the db.ts fixture.\n329\t\n330\t## File List\n331\t\n332\t- `server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map.ex` (modified — inspector controls, off-endpoint list, capture enable/disable handlers, cascade logic)\n333\t- `server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_status.ex` (modified if needed — off-tier status handling)\n334\t- `server/priv/static/assets/app.js` (modified — `TraceMapLeitstand` hook extended with countdown timer and capture state)\n335\t- `server/assets/css/app.css` (modified or verified — off/temp visual states)\n336\t- `server/test/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_leitstand_test.exs` (extended — inspector controls, capture logic)\n337\t- `server/priv/migrations/XXXXXXX_admin_temporary_captures_table.up.sql` (new — create `admin_temporary_captures` table)\n338\t- `server/priv/migrations/XXXXXXX_admin_temporary_captures_table.down.sql` (new)\n339\t- `e2e/features/admin/logs-trace-map.feature` (extended — 6 new scenarios A–F)\n340\t- `e2e/step-definitions/admin/logs-trace-map.steps.ts` (extended — step implementations for capture scenarios)\n341\t- `docs/stories/enhanced-logging/noise-endpoint-bucketing.md` (marked as superseded, with note to reuse backend constant)\n342\t\n343\t## Summary\n344\t\n345\tThis story completes the Trace Map Leitstand by making the inspector panel an interactive logging control center. Per-endpoint collection levels and retention windows are now configurable from the map itself (wired to the existing retention-settings backend). Long-polling endpoints (sync, keepalive, health) are off by default — gray, dashed, no data — keeping the signal-to-noise ratio high. A single click enables temporary 5-minute logging captures with server-side timers, countdown countdowns, and automatic expiry. The backend concept for identifying low-signal endpoints is reused from the superseded `noise-endpoint-bucketing` story, ensuring a single source of truth for future stories.\n346\t\n347\t---\n348\t\n349\t# Acceptance Criteria Summary\n350\t\n351\t| AC | Title | Type |\n352\t|----|----|------|\n353\t| 1 | Inspector Min Level Select | Feature |\n354\t| 2 | Inspector Retention Select | Feature |\n355\t| 3 | Inspector Built Once, Stats Update Only | Constraint |\n356\t| 4 | Long-Polling Off-by-Default — Config-Driven | Feature |\n357\t| 5 | Click Off-Node Enables 5-Min Capture | Feature |\n358\t| 6 | Countdown Display mm:ss | Feature |\n359\t| 7 | While Capturing, Filter Works Normally | Behavior |\n360\t| 8 | Visual States Off/Temp | Visual |\n361\t| 9 | Accessibility — Labels, Keyboard, Announcements | A11y |\n362\t| 10 | CSP & Security Compliance | Security |\n363\t| 11 | E2E Test Coverage — 6 Scenarios | Test |\n364\t| 12 | Persistence & Audit — Capture Actions | Policy |\n365\t| 13 | Superseding noise-endpoint-bucketing | Coordination |\n366\t\n367\t**Total: 13 ACs**\n368\t\n369\t## Story Amendments (2026-07-07, coordinator/design owner)\n370\t\n371\t- **One-click capture enable confirmed** (ATDD flag 1): clicking an off node enables\n372\t  capture immediately (dummy behavior, AC5/AC7 contract); the inspector \"Enable\" button\n373\t  exists for the post-early-disable state only. The intermediate click-then-Enable flow\n374\t  from the original AC text is superseded.\n375\t- **Retention options** (ATDD flag 4): the select offers **1 d / 7 d / 30 d**, mapping\n376\t  to the existing day-granularity retention backend. The dummy's \"1 h\" option is\n377\t  dropped — hour-granularity retention is out of scope for this story.\n378\t"}]},"uuid":"8826a6d4-bfbf-40e6-a961-273ab3b0f9f3","timestamp":"2026-07-07T18:52:46.595Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/docs/stories/enhanced-logging/trace-map-node-controls.md","content":"---\nid: trace-map-node-controls\ntitle: \"Trace Map Node Controls — inspector logging panel + long-polling off-by-default with temporary capture\"\nepic: enhanced-logging\nstatus: ready-for-dev\nsecurity_review: required\nmatrix: false\nui: true\ncreated: 2026-07-07\n---\n\n# Trace Map Node Controls — Per-Node Logging Inspector & Temporary Capture\n\n## Story\n\n**As** a Nebu platform operator managing production logs,\n**I want** the Trace Map inspector to become a logging control panel where I can configure per-endpoint collection levels and retention, and enable temporary logging on long-polling endpoints that are off by default,\n**so that** I can fine-tune collection policy without leaving the Trace Map view, safely experiment with high-frequency endpoints via time-limited capture, and keep the map signal-to-noise ratio high by defaulting polling endpoints to off.\n\nThis story completes the approved Leitstand design (spec §4 and §5 from `_bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/design/trace-map-leitstand-spec.md`), building on the already-shipped `trace-map-leitstand` story. The inspector panel, previously read-only with live stats only, becomes an interactive logging control center. Long-polling endpoints (config-driven list, default: `/_matrix/client/v3/sync`, `/admin/keepalive`, `/health`) are rendered off by default — gray, dashed, count \"off\", no collection — and a single click enables TEMPORARY capture for 5 minutes with a server-side timer, countdown display, auto-revert, and aria-live announcements.\n\n**This story SUPERSEDES the UI concept of `noise-endpoint-bucketing.md`** (status: ready-for-dev, with a \"Design Update — REALIGNMENT REQUIRED\" note): the bucketing-UI (collapse/expand category) is obsolete; the backend concept (config-driven low-signal endpoint list, off-by-default collection) is reused. See AC13 for the coordination note.\n\n### Design Source\n\nAuthoritative design: `_bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/design/trace-map-leitstand-spec.md` (Phil approved 2026-07-07).\n- §4: Inspector control panel (logging controls)\n- §5: Long-polling endpoints — off by default, temporary capture with countdown\n- §8: Hard implementation constraints (cluster-safe timers, no node-local Process timers)\n\nVisual/interaction reference: `trace-map-leitstand-dummy.html` (interactive browser dummy), sections \"Inspector\" and gray-dashed off-nodes with countdown.\n\n**Out of scope:** The live-stream panel (already shipped in `trace-map-leitstand`); other monitoring features.\n\n## Acceptance Criteria\n\n### AC1: Inspector Logging Controls — Min Level Select\n\nWhen a node is inspected (focused/clicked on the Trace Map), the inspector panel below the live stats renders a \"Logging\" section with a **Min level select** (DEBUG/INFO/WARN/ERROR):\n- The select is wired to the per-endpoint collection level from `admin-logs-retention-settings` (already done)\n- On mount/focus, the select is pre-populated with the endpoint's current configured level from the server_config\n- Changing the select triggers a server round-trip to persist the new level (via the existing `Nebu.Admin.DB.upsert_server_config/1` API)\n- **For domain/nebu nodes**: the label reads \"Applies to all endpoints in this domain\" (or \"on the map\" for nebu); the setting cascades to all descendant endpoints as a batch operation\n- **For satellite/endpoint nodes**: the label reads \"Applies to this endpoint only\"\n- The select never loses focus or value during re-renders (pinned by a test: the panel is built once per inspected node, only stat values update per tick)\n\n### AC2: Inspector Logging Controls — Retention Select\n\nWhen a node is inspected, the \"Logging\" section also renders a **Retention select** (1 h / 24 h / 7 d / 30 d):\n- Wired to the per-endpoint retention days from `admin-logs-retention-settings`\n- Pre-populated from server_config on mount/focus\n- Changing the select persists the new retention window\n- Same cascade rules as AC1 (domain/nebu cascade; endpoint is solo)\n- Same focus-preservation guarantee\n\n### AC3: Inspector Built Once Per Node, Only Stats Update\n\nThe inspector panel HTML structure and form elements are BUILT once when a node is inspected (focused/clicked). On every subsequent stat update (new log entry, window slide), only the **stat values** (request count, warning count, error count, last 5 events) are patched via LiveView assigns — not the entire panel markup.\n\n**Test pinned:** Click node → panel renders → assert select `value` and `focus` are correct. Inject log entry → stat patch happens → assert select still has same `value` and is still focused (if it was before).\n\n### AC4: Long-Polling Off-by-Default — Config-Driven List\n\nA config-driven list of long-polling endpoints (default: `/_matrix/client/v3/sync`, `/admin/keepalive`, `/health`) are rendered as **logging-off nodes**:\n- **Rendered state**: gray fill (base-200 @ 15%), text-disabled dashed 1.5px stroke, count shows \"off\" text (not a number), no status color, no error/warn badges\n- **Data not collected**: entries for these endpoints are NOT persisted to `operational_logs` (server-side enforcement via policy), and the status window shows zero requests/warnings/errors\n- **No status propagation**: a parent domain with ALL children as off-endpoints shows no status (no error/warn/ok; just gray and dashed like its children)\n- **Backend concept reused from noise-endpoint-bucketing**: a hardcoded constant (or config key) defines which endpoints are in the off-by-default list; the same list is used by all consuming stories (reuse potential for future war-room-dedicated-page)\n- **Configuration**: the list is defined as `@long_polling_endpoints = [...]` (or similar) in `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap`, with clear documentation; a public function `def long_polling_endpoints()` exposes it for reuse\n\n### AC5: Click Off-Node Enables Temporary Capture (5 Minutes)\n\nClicking on an off-tier endpoint enables **temporary capture** for 5 minutes:\n- Server-side timer: a stateful capture record is inserted into a temporary store (e.g., `admin_temporary_captures` table, or a volatile in-memory structure backed by a periodic cleanup task), recording `endpoint`, `enabled_at`, `expires_at` (now + 5 minutes), and `started_by` (current admin user)\n- **NOT client-only state**: the capture timer is enforced server-side; no client-side `setTimeout` that breaks on page reload/crash\n- **Cluster-safe**: if the deployment is multi-node, the temporary capture record is in the shared database or a distributed cache (follow the pattern used by `war_room_manager` — design decision notes below)\n- While capturing, the node becomes **live** with dashed status stroke + countdown on the node and in the inspector panel\n- Visual state: \"temp capture\" from the spec (as status, dashed stroke, countdown mm:ss)\n- Log entries for the endpoint are now collected (the policy check skips the off-list while capture is active)\n- **Early disable button**: the inspector panel shows \"Disable now\" button (when capture is active), which manually ends the capture before expiry\n- **Auto-revert**: when the timer expires, the endpoint reverts to off automatically with no manual intervention\n- **Aria-live announcements**: enable event announces \"Capture enabled for X endpoint — 5 minutes\" (or similar); disable/expiry announce \"Capture ended\" or \"5 minutes expired\"\n\n### AC6: Temporary Capture Countdown Display\n\nWhen capture is active on a node:\n- The node itself displays countdown mm:ss (e.g., \"04:32\" for 4 minutes 32 seconds remaining)\n- The inspector panel displays the same countdown + \"Disable now\" button\n- Countdown updates live every second (client-side, computed from server's `expires_at` — no server tick needed; the expiry time is set once, client updates every 1 s via JS hook)\n- On expiry, the countdown disappears and the node reverts to off state (gray + dashed, count \"off\", no countdown)\n\n### AC7: While Capturing, Stream Filter Works Normally\n\nWhile a node is in temporary-capture state:\n- A second click (or Enter/Space key) toggles the node's inclusion in the stream filter (same as any other node)\n- The capture timer continues regardless of filter state\n- Clicking \"Disable now\" in the inspector ends the capture (node reverts to off, filter is NOT automatically removed if active)\n- This allows the operator to enable capture, then immediately filter by that endpoint to watch its logs, then disable capture early if they see what they need\n\n### AC8: Visual States — Off (Logging-Off) and Temp (Temporary Capture)\n\nTwo new visual states, defined in the Trace Map legend (already rendered in `trace-map-leitstand`):\n- **Off**: base-200 @ 15% fill, text-disabled dashed 1.5px stroke, \"off\" text label, no status color\n- **Temp**: status color (ok/warn/error) fill, status color dashed stroke, countdown mm:ss on the node, no badges (until capture ends and status is evaluated)\n\nStatus is never color-alone; the \"off\" text, countdown, and aria-labels make the state explicit.\n\n### AC9: Accessibility — Labels, Keyboard, Announcements\n\n- Inspector selects (min level, retention) are labelled with `<label for=\"...\">` elements\n- Clicking an off-node, pressing Enter/Space on a focused off-node, or clicking \"Disable now\" dispatches aria-live announcements:\n  - Enable: \"Temporary capture enabled for [endpoint name] · 5 minutes\"\n  - Disable: \"Temporary capture disabled for [endpoint name]\"\n  - Expiry: \"Temporary capture expired for [endpoint name]\"\n- Aria-labels on off-nodes read \"off\" (e.g., \"sync endpoint · /_matrix/client/v3/sync · logging off\")\n- Aria-labels on temp-capture nodes include countdown (e.g., \"sync endpoint · /_matrix/client/v3/sync · temporary capture · 4 minutes 32 seconds remaining\")\n- \"Disable now\" button has `aria-label=\"Disable capture for sync endpoint\"` or similar\n\n### AC10: CSP & Security Compliance\n\n- No inline JS; countdown updating via app.js `TraceMapLeitstand` hook (existing hook from `trace-map-leitstand`, extended with capture-state awareness)\n- Inspector form: POST to a new server action or a LiveView event (`handle_event(\"configure_logging\", ...)`)\n- Capture enable/disable: POST/DELETE or `handle_event(\"enable_capture\", ...)` / `handle_event(\"disable_capture\", ...)` on the LiveView\n- CSRF token included in any form\n- No raw endpoint names exposed in URLs (filter state is already URL-safe per `trace-map-leitstand`'s filter-state design)\n\n### AC11: E2E Test Coverage — Inspector Controls & Capture\n\nNew BDD scenarios (in `e2e/features/admin/logs-trace-map.feature`, or a new file if preferred):\n\n**Scenario A: Inspector loads with endpoint's current logging config**\n- Given I click an endpoint node on the Trace Map\n- Then the inspector panel shows the endpoint's current min level and retention from the config\n- When I change the min level to DEBUG\n- Then a server round-trip persists the change\n- And the inspector panel's level select still has focus (pinned: focus is not lost)\n\n**Scenario B: Cascade — changing domain node config applies to all satellites**\n- Given I click a domain node (e.g., \"Matrix Client\")\n- Then the inspector panel's level select shows \"Applies to all endpoints in this domain\"\n- When I change the level to WARN\n- Then all satellites under the domain inherit WARN level (verified via a second page load or a Playwright DB query)\n- And a success message appears (\"Updated X endpoints\")\n\n**Scenario C: Off-endpoint enable capture**\n- Given an off-endpoint (e.g., \"/health\") is rendered as gray + dashed with count \"off\"\n- When I click the node\n- Then the inspector panel shows \"Logging is off\" + \"Enable capture for 5 minutes\" button (or similar)\n- When I click \"Enable capture\"\n- Then the node becomes live with countdown mm:ss (e.g., \"04:59\")\n- And aria-live announces \"Temporary capture enabled for /health · 5 minutes\"\n- And the countdown updates every second (visual test: verify 04:59 → 04:58 after ~1 second)\n\n**Scenario D: Countdown expires and auto-reverts**\n- Given capture is active on an off-endpoint with countdown visible\n- When I wait for the countdown to reach 00:00 (or simulate via server-side time-travel if testable)\n- Then the node reverts to off state (gray + dashed, count \"off\", no countdown)\n- And aria-live announces \"Temporary capture expired for /health\"\n\n**Scenario E: Early disable**\n- Given capture is active with countdown \"02:15\"\n- When I click \"Disable now\" in the inspector panel\n- Then the node immediately reverts to off\n- And aria-live announces \"Temporary capture disabled for /health\"\n\n**Scenario F: While capturing, filter toggle works**\n- Given capture is active on \"/sync\"\n- When I click the node again (while capturing)\n- Then the node shows a selection ring (filter is toggled on)\n- When the countdown expires\n- Then the node reverts to off (filter may remain active or be cleared, per design decision in Dev Notes)\n- And the stream continues to show entries if the filter was active\n\n### AC12: Persistence & Audit — Capture Enable/Disable Actions\n\n**Design decision point (to be clarified in Dev Notes):**\n\n**Option A (No audit log entry):** Temporary capture enable/disable are not recorded in the compliance audit log. They are user-facing logging experiments, not configuration changes.\n\n**Option B (Admin actions in audit log):** Temporary capture enable/disable are recorded as admin actions in `compliance_audit_log` (similar to how `admin-logs-retention-settings` records setting changes), with the format: `action: \"enable_temporary_capture\" / \"disable_temporary_capture\"`, `resource_type: \"endpoint\"`, `resource_id: endpoint_name`, `admin_user: current_user`, `timestamp: now()`.\n\n**For this story**, adopt **Option A (no audit)** as the baseline unless the security review mandates otherwise. The rationale: temporary captures are transient experiments (5 min expiry), not persistent configuration changes, and they're already logged implicitly by the operational_logs themselves (entries that wouldn't normally be persisted are being persisted, which is visible in the logs store).\n\n### AC13: Superseding noise-endpoint-bucketing Story\n\nThe story `docs/stories/enhanced-logging/noise-endpoint-bucketing.md` (status: ready-for-dev) proposed a \"Polling/Keepalive\" bucket/collapse-expand UI in the Trace Map's endpoint dimension. Phil's Leitstand redesign supersedes that UI concept.\n\n**This story supersedes that story's implementation scope by:**\n1. Marking `noise-endpoint-bucketing` as status: `superseded` (with a note: \"UI concept merged into `trace-map-node-controls` — reuse backend constant\")\n2. Reusing the backend concept: the canonical constant `@long_polling_endpoints` (or `@noise_endpoints`, using the naming from the superseded story for continuity) is the same list the superseded story would have used\n3. Documenting the deprecation in `noise-endpoint-bucketing.md`'s frontmatter and a note in this story's Dev Notes\n\n**The PM/coordinator should:**\n- Mark `noise-endpoint-bucketing` as status `superseded` in the planning artifact\n- No implementation work is needed for that story (it is merged into this one)\n\n## Dev Notes\n\n### Files to Create/Modify\n\n**Modify:**\n- `server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map.ex` (add inspector logging controls, off-endpoint list, capture timer logic, event handlers)\n- `server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_status.ex` (may need to handle off-endpoints differently if they should show zero in the status window)\n- `server/priv/static/assets/app.js` (`TraceMapLeitstand` hook extended: countdown timer updates, \"Disable now\" click handler)\n- `server/assets/css/app.css` (visual states for off/temp if not already covered by `trace-map-leitstand`)\n- `server/test/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_leitstand_test.exs` (extend existing tests to cover new inspector controls and capture logic)\n- `e2e/features/admin/logs-trace-map.feature` (add 6 new scenarios A–F above)\n- `e2e/step-definitions/admin/logs-trace-map.steps.ts` (step implementations for capture scenarios)\n\n**Create (if not already in place):**\n- Migration or seed script: ensure `admin_temporary_captures` table exists (if using a DB-backed timer; see design decision below). Schema: `endpoint TEXT`, `enabled_at TIMESTAMP`, `expires_at TIMESTAMP`, `started_by TEXT`, `primary_key: (endpoint, started_by)` (exactly one active capture per endpoint per user, or per endpoint globally depending on design choice)\n\n### Design Decisions — Cluster-Safe Temporary Capture Timer\n\n**Problem:** A naive `Process.send_after(self(), {:revert_to_off, endpoint}, 5 * 60 * 1000)` only works on a single node. In a multi-node deployment, when the process crashes or the node is restarted, the timer is lost.\n\n**Solution:** Follow `Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager`'s pattern:\n1. **Insert a record into a temporary captures table** (`admin_temporary_captures` or similar):\n   - `endpoint TEXT PRIMARY KEY` (only one active capture per endpoint)\n   - `enabled_at TIMESTAMP` (server time when capture started)\n   - `expires_at TIMESTAMP` (server time = now + 5 minutes)\n   - `started_by TEXT` (admin user who enabled it)\n2. **Server-side enforcement at ingest time:** When a log entry arrives for an off-list endpoint, check if a record exists in the temporary captures table with `expires_at > now()`. If yes, persist the entry; if no, skip it (off-tier enforcement).\n3. **Cleanup on a timer:** A periodic task (e.g., `Nebu.Admin.TemporaryCapturesCleaner` or integrated into the existing `Nebu.Web.LogStore.Sweeper`) runs every 10–30 seconds and deletes expired records (`DELETE FROM admin_temporary_captures WHERE expires_at < now()`).\n4. **LiveView listener:** The LiveView's `handle_info/2` also checks the table on every PubSub message and patches nodes' state if a capture has expired.\n\n**Outcome:** Cluster-safe, survives node restarts, deterministic (server time, not wall-clock).\n\n**Alternative:** Use Redis for the temporary captures table (if Redis is in the deployment). Same logic, just swap the table for a Redis key with TTL. Not recommended unless Redis is mandatory anyway.\n\n**For testing:** Make the 5-minute duration configurable via an Application env var (e.g., `Application.get_env(:nebu, :temporary_capture_duration_s, 300)`), so tests can use a much shorter duration (e.g., 1 second) without waiting 5 minutes. The same pattern is used by `war_room_manager` (AC8/AC10 in `war-room-mode` story).\n\n### Architecture Notes\n\n**Inspector Control Panel:**\n- The panel is rendered in `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap.render(:trace_map)`, below the live stats section\n- The two selects (`<select name=\"min_level\">`, `<select name=\"retention_days\">`) are part of the inspector panel component\n- Each select's `name` attribute is encoded with the inspected node's key (e.g., `min_level[/sync]`, `retention_days[/_matrix/client]`) to distinguish which node's config is being changed\n- Or simpler: use a single form per inspector panel rebuild, with a hidden field storing the inspected node's key, and the selects are always for that one node\n- On change event (phx-change or phx-submit), the handler extracts the node key and the new value, and calls the same backend that `admin-logs-retention-settings` uses to persist: `Nebu.Admin.DB.upsert_server_config/1` with a map like `%{\"log_min_level_/sync\" => :debug, \"log_retention_days_/sync\" => 7}`\n\n**Off-Endpoint List & Capture Logic:**\n- Define `@long_polling_endpoints = [...]` in `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap`\n- A public function `def long_polling_endpoints()` returns it (for reuse by other modules)\n- In the Trace Map's render, filter nodes to check `Enum.member?(@long_polling_endpoints, node.key)` to determine if the node is off-tier\n- In `handle_event(\"enable_capture\", ...)` or similar, insert a record into `admin_temporary_captures` and broadcast a PubSub message to update the LiveView\n- The countdown display is computed client-side in the `TraceMapLeitstand` hook: `Math.max(0, (expires_at_ms - Date.now()) / 1000)` every second\n\n**Cascade for Domain/Nebu Nodes:**\n- If the inspected node is a domain or nebu, the select change triggers a batch operation: instead of a single `upsert_server_config`, call a helper (e.g., `apply_config_to_descendants(node_key, new_config)`) that updates all satellite endpoints under the domain (or all endpoints on the map, for nebu)\n- Implementation: for each descendant, call `upsert_server_config` in a transaction or a loop\n- Success flash: \"Updated X endpoints\" (where X is the count of affected satellites)\n\n**Focus Preservation on Inspector Panel:**\n- The inspector panel is rendered as a separate component (or template section) that is only re-rendered when the inspected node changes (not on every stat update)\n- Use Phoenix.LiveView's `assign/3` for stat updates only; stat updates should NOT trigger a re-render of the selects\n- In Playwright tests, verify that `await page.locator('select[name=\"min_level\"]').focus()` persists across stat updates (LiveView patches the stat values without touching the select element's DOM)\n\n### Testing Strategy\n\n**Unit tests** (`logs_live/trace_map_leitstand_test.exs`):\n- Mount with an off-tier endpoint present → verify gray + dashed rendering, count shows \"off\"\n- Click off-tier endpoint → enable capture → verify node state changes to temp, countdown appears\n- Wait for expiry (or simulate via time-warp in test) → verify node reverts to off\n- Click \"Disable now\" → verify immediate revert\n- Inspector selects: change level → verify server round-trip and persistence\n- Domain node: change level → verify batch update of all satellites (query DB to confirm)\n- Focus preservation: click node → select gets focus → inject log entry (handle_info) → verify select still focused\n\n**Integration tests** (same file):\n- Full-stack: enable capture → check `admin_temporary_captures` table → verify entry exists\n- Cleanup timer: insert expired record → run cleanup task → verify record deleted\n- PubSub broadcast: enable capture on one page → another page listening → verify state updates without reload\n\n**E2E tests** (`logs-trace-map.feature`):\n- Scenarios A–F above, using Playwright to interact with UI and await countdown updates\n- No sleeps: use `await page.waitForTimeout(1000)` or similar for the 1-second countdown update, or use `await page.waitForFunction(...)` to wait until countdown text changes\n\n### Reuse Points for Future Stories\n\n1. **Canonical noise endpoint list:** `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.long_polling_endpoints()` is the single source of truth for \"which endpoints are low-signal\" — reused by any future story that needs to identify polling endpoints (e.g., war-room-dedicated-page's \"safe scope\" option, trace-map-hierarchy's domain-grouping logic)\n2. **Temporary capture pattern:** The `admin_temporary_captures` table and cleanup task provide a reusable template for other time-limited operations (though temporary capture itself is specific to this story)\n3. **Inspector panel pattern:** The read-once-update-stats approach (panel built once per node, only stat values patched) is a good pattern for any inspector-like panel that needs to preserve form state during live updates\n\n### Security Notes\n\n- **Sensitive data:** Off-tier endpoints are still endpoints (routes), not payloads. Rendering them as \"off\" is transparent (they appear in the legend, in the map, on the inspector label). No sensitive data leakage.\n- **Capture enable/disable:** Requires admin auth (inherited from the LogsLive's `:admin` gate). No open API.\n- **Temporary capture duration:** Hardcoded to 5 minutes, enforced server-side, with a configurable test override. Operators cannot arbitrarily extend or remove the timer.\n- **Audit (Option A chosen):** No compliance log entries, but the operations themselves are opaque to the operator (they enable capture, entries appear in `operational_logs`, they can read them). If a compliance review later requires audit entries, Option B can be added in a follow-up story.\n\n## Known Challenges & Design Decisions\n\n1. **Off-tier status propagation:** A parent domain with ALL children as off-tier (e.g., `/admin` with only `/admin/keepalive` as its satellite) should itself be off-tier (gray + dashed). Implement this by checking in the status-propagation logic: if all children are off, parent is off; otherwise, parent inherits worst-status from capturing children.\n\n2. **Capture expiry visibility:** The expiry is server-side, but the countdown display is client-side. If the browser tab is closed and reopened during a capture, the page loads fresh; on mount, the LiveView checks the `admin_temporary_captures` table and re-renders the node in temp-capture state with an updated countdown. This requires the `expires_at` timestamp to be sent to the client on every mount (or on every stat update, if the page is already loaded).\n\n3. **Multiple endpoints, multiple captures:** If the operator enables capture on two off-tier endpoints simultaneously, two rows exist in `admin_temporary_captures`. Each countdown is independent. Cleanup runs periodically and deletes both when their respective expiry times are reached. No special ordering or batching needed.\n\n4. **Filter state during capture:** If the operator enables capture on `/sync`, then filters by `/sync`, then disables capture early, the filter remains active (nodes can be filtered and captured independently). The stream continues to show `/sync` entries because the filter is active. Disabling capture does NOT clear filters (even if the node becomes off-tier again, it stays in the filter state).\n\n## References & Cross-Story Coordination\n\n- **Depends on:** `trace-map-leitstand` (shipped, provides the base Leitstand page and inspector panel structure)\n- **Depends on:** `admin-logs-retention-settings` (shipped, provides config storage and API for log level/retention)\n- **Blocks:** None (standalone feature)\n- **Supersedes:** `noise-endpoint-bucketing` (UI concept merged into this story; backend concept reused)\n- **Coordinates with:** `war-room-dedicated-page` (potential future reuse of the `long_polling_endpoints` constant for war-room scopes)\n- **Coordinates with:** `trace-map-hierarchy` (potential future integration of off-tier endpoints into the hierarchy tree)\n\n## Definition of Ready\n\nAll ACs are present and testable. The story is ready for development once:\n1. Security review confirms the temporary capture timer design is acceptable (or recommends an alternative)\n2. PM clarifies AC12 (audit log: Option A or Option B) if a preference exists\n3. Coordinates with `noise-endpoint-bucketing` to mark it as superseded\n\n## Story Amendments\n\n(None at time of creation.)\n\n## Questions for Dev Agent — ANSWERED (coordinator/design owner, 2026-07-07)\n\n1. **Cluster-safe timer design: database-backed** (`expires_at` timestamp column; state is a row, expiry is a comparison against now() — no process timer owns correctness). Follow the war_room_manager precedent for the polling/refresh side. Rationale: Nebu is compliance-native and self-hosted with multi-node ambitions (cluster-safety convention); in-memory captures silently outliving or vanishing across deploys/nodes is exactly the class of bug this epic keeps meeting.\n\n2. **AC12 audit logging: Option B — YES, audit-log capture enable/disable.** Enabling collection on a previously-off endpoint is an admin-initiated change to logging policy (same category as retention-settings changes — follow that story's audit precedent). Kassandra reviews this story; Option B preempts the obvious finding.\n\n3. **Capture duration via `Application.get_env(:nebu, :temporary_capture_duration_s, 300)`: approved** (war_room_manager pattern). E2E may alternatively seed an about-to-expire capture row via the db.ts fixture.\n\n## File List\n\n- `server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map.ex` (modified — inspector controls, off-endpoint list, capture enable/disable handlers, cascade logic)\n- `server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_status.ex` (modified if needed — off-tier status handling)\n- `server/priv/static/assets/app.js` (modified — `TraceMapLeitstand` hook extended with countdown timer and capture state)\n- `server/assets/css/app.css` (modified or verified — off/temp visual states)\n- `server/test/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_leitstand_test.exs` (extended — inspector controls, capture logic)\n- `server/priv/migrations/XXXXXXX_admin_temporary_captures_table.up.sql` (new — create `admin_temporary_captures` table)\n- `server/priv/migrations/XXXXXXX_admin_temporary_captures_table.down.sql` (new)\n- `e2e/features/admin/logs-trace-map.feature` (extended — 6 new scenarios A–F)\n- `e2e/step-definitions/admin/logs-trace-map.steps.ts` (extended — step implementations for capture scenarios)\n- `docs/stories/enhanced-logging/noise-endpoint-bucketing.md` (marked as superseded, with note to reuse backend constant)\n\n## Summary\n\nThis story completes the Trace Map Leitstand by making the inspector panel an interactive logging control center. Per-endpoint collection levels and retention windows are now configurable from the map itself (wired to the existing retention-settings backend). Long-polling endpoints (sync, keepalive, health) are off by default — gray, dashed, no data — keeping the signal-to-noise ratio high. A single click enables temporary 5-minute logging captures with server-side timers, countdown countdowns, and automatic expiry. The backend concept for identifying low-signal endpoints is reused from the superseded `noise-endpoint-bucketing` story, ensuring a single source of truth for future stories.\n\n---\n\n# Acceptance Criteria Summary\n\n| AC | Title | Type |\n|----|----|------|\n| 1 | Inspector Min Level Select | Feature |\n| 2 | Inspector Retention Select | Feature |\n| 3 | Inspector Built Once, Stats Update Only | Constraint |\n| 4 | Long-Polling Off-by-Default — Config-Driven | Feature |\n| 5 | Click Off-Node Enables 5-Min Capture | Feature |\n| 6 | Countdown Display mm:ss | Feature |\n| 7 | While Capturing, Filter Works Normally | Behavior |\n| 8 | Visual States Off/Temp | Visual |\n| 9 | Accessibility — Labels, Keyboard, Announcements | A11y |\n| 10 | CSP & Security Compliance | Security |\n| 11 | E2E Test Coverage — 6 Scenarios | Test |\n| 12 | Persistence & Audit — Capture Actions | Policy |\n| 13 | Superseding noise-endpoint-bucketing | Coordination |\n\n**Total: 13 ACs**\n\n## Story Amendments (2026-07-07, coordinator/design owner)\n\n- **One-click capture enable confirmed** (ATDD flag 1): clicking an off node enables\n  capture immediately (dummy behavior, AC5/AC7 contract); the inspector \"Enable\" button\n  exists for the post-early-disable state only. The intermediate click-then-Enable flow\n  from the original AC text is superseded.\n- **Retention options** (ATDD flag 4): the select offers **1 d / 7 d / 30 d**, mapping\n  to the existing day-granularity retention backend. The dummy's \"1 h\" option is\n  dropped — hour-granularity retention is out of scope for this story.\n","numLines":378,"startLine":1,"totalLines":378}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"449ed9f4-f839-4a17-a8ac-d84693137852","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

NO test-only HTTP routes.\n12\t\n13\t## Red confirmation\n14\t- `make test-unit-elixir`: **1499/1533 passed, 34 failures, 2 skipped, 388 excluded.** All 34 failures are in the two new test modules (19 contract + 15 LiveView). Integration-tagged tests (11 + 8) excluded here — red at the CI integration gate (`admin_temporary_captures` table does not exist).\n15\t- `npx bddgen`: ok (default tags — pending scenarios excluded); `NEBU_E2E_TAGS='@pending-feature' npx bddgen`: ok, generates 6 tests for the new feature file.\n16\t\n17\t## Contract pinned for the dev agent (full detail in the test moduledocs)\n18\t- New module `Nebu.Admin.TemporaryCaptureManager` (WarRoomManager precedent, ADR-018/020); table `admin_temporary_captures` (endpoint TEXT PK, enabled_at/expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ, started_by TEXT); expected migration number **000020**.\n19\t- `should_persist?/2 (level, route_template)` — off-list route + no capture ⇒ false for ALL levels (even errors, AC4 literal); capture active ⇒ delegate to level policy; non-off/nil route ⇒ delegate.\n20\t- Config keys: `log_min_level_<route>` (lowercase value) / `log_retention_days_<route>` via `upsert_server_config/1`; change event `configure_logging` with params `node_key`/`min_level`/`retention_days`; events `enable_capture`/`disable_capture`.\n21\t- Node states: `data-node-status=\"off\"|\"temp\"`; countdown `[data-testid=\"trace-map-node-countdown\"]` mm:ss with `data-expires-at` (unix s, war-room `data-started-at` precedent). Inspector: `trace-map-inspector-logging`/`-level-select`/`-retention-select`/`-scope-note`/`-logging-form`/`-capture-state`/`-capture-toggle`/`-capture-countdown` testids; dummy copy (\"Logging off (long-polling)\", \"Enable for 5 min\", \"Capture active\", \"Disable now\").\n22\t- Off endpoints are rendered FROM MOUNT (they collect no data, so they can never appear by observation); their broadcasts are excluded from status window, stream panel, and nebu aggregation; all-children-off ⇒ domain off.\n23\t- AuditWriter `@known_actions` must gain `enable_temporary_capture`/`disable_temporary_capture` (Option B, answered design Q2).\n24\t\n25\t## Deviations / flags for dev + PM\n26\t1. **Scenario C story-vs-dummy discrepancy (resolved for the dummy + AC5/AC7):** AC11 Scenario C prose sketches a two-step enable (click node → button → capture); the approved dummy's `activate()` + AC5 headline + AC7's \"second click\" pin ONE-CLICK enable. Tests follow one-click; the inspector's \"Enable for 5 min\" button is still pinned (reachable after early disable — LiveView test + E2E Scenario E). PM to confirm.\n27\t2. **AC4 \"not even errors\":** off-list enforcement blocks ERROR-level persistence too (AC4's literal \"entries are NOT persisted\"). Deliberate pin — flag if ops wants errors always collected.\n28\t3. **Cascade count not pinned to \"2\":** `/admin/keepalive` (always-rendered off endpoint) may legitimately join the `/admin` cascade set; flash asserted as `Updated \\d+ endpoints`. Dev decides inclusion.\n29\t4. **Retention option \"1 h\" vs `log_retention_days_*` key:** the dummy's 1-hour option cannot be an integer day count — tests only pin the \"7 d\"→\"7\" and \"30 d\"→\"30\" mappings. test written for a docs file; the reuse half of AC13 is pinned via `long_polling_endpoints/0`.\n31\t6. **Expiry-while-open unit test uses a 1.2 s sleep** with `temporary_capture_duration_s = 1` (env-config path blessed by answered design Q3); every other expiry test seeds already-expired/about-to-expire rows — no 5-minute waits anywhere.\n32\t7. **Audit assertions query `audit_log` directly** (integration) — a new but straightforward pattern; existing audit tests only use FakeRepo injection (also used here for the vocabulary pin).\n33\t\n34\t## AC coverage map\n35\t| AC | Pre-existing baseline of 1498 fully green. 1 new test deliberately green (AC10 inline-JS regression guard, documented in-file; leitstand precedent). Integration-tagged tests (11 + 8) excluded here — red at the CI integration gate (`admin_temporary_captures` table does not exist).\n- `npx bddgen`: ok (default tags — pending scenarios excluded); `NEBU_E2E_TAGS='@pending-feature' npx bddgen`: ok, generates 6 tests for the new feature file.\n\n## Contract pinned for the dev agent (full detail in the test moduledocs)\n- New module `Nebu.Admin.TemporaryCaptureManager` (WarRoomManager precedent, ADR-018/020); table `admin_temporary_captures` (endpoint TEXT PK, enabled_at/expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ, started_by TEXT); expected migration number **000020**.\n- `should_persist?/2 (level, route_template)` — off-list route + no capture ⇒ false for ALL levels (even errors, AC4 literal); capture active ⇒ delegate to level policy; non-off/nil route ⇒ delegate.\n- Config keys: `log_min_level_<route>` (lowercase value) / `log_retention_days_<route>` via `upsert_server_config/1`; change event `configure_logging` with params `node_key`/`min_level`/`retention_days`; events `enable_capture`/`disable_capture`.\n- Node states: `data-node-status=\"off\"|\"temp\"`; countdown `[data-testid=\"trace-map-node-countdown\"]` mm:ss with `data-expires-at` (unix s, war-room `data-started-at` precedent). Contract pinned by this file\n35\t\n36\t    * AC4/AC13: `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap.long_polling_endpoints/0` is\n37\t      the canonical, reusable off-by-default list (the superseded\n38\t      `noise-endpoint-bucketing` story's backend concept) — default members\n39\t      exactly `/_matrix/client/v3/sync`, `/admin/keepalive`, `/health`.\n40\t      `off_endpoint?/1` is the membership predicate.\n41\t    * AC1/AC2 cascade: `cascade_targets(node_key, endpoint_order)` is a PURE\n42\t      function (ADR-018/021 plain module style) computing which observed\n43\t      endpoint route templates a config change on `node_key` applies to:\n44\t      the endpoint itself for a satellite, every endpoint of the domain for\n45\t      a domain key (via `TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint/1`, never a\n46\t      duplicated heuristic), every observed endpoint for `\"nebu\"`.\n47\t    * AC5/AC6 (ANSWERED design question 1: DB-backed timer — \"state is a\n48\t      row, expiry is a comparison against now()\"):\n49\t      `Nebu.Admin.TemporaryCaptureManager.expired?(capture, now)` and\n50\t      `remaining_seconds(capture, now)` are pure comparisons over the\n51\t      capture row's `:expires_at` `DateTime` — no process timer owns\n52\t      correctness. `capture_duration_s/0` reads\n53\t      `Application.get_env(:nebu, :temporary_capture_duration_s, 300)`\n54\t      (ANSWERED design question 3, war_room_manager precedent).\n55\t      `active?/1` is guarded behind `Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo)` (the same\n56\t      DB-free-unit-test contract as `initial_war_room_status/0`) and\n57\t      answers `false` when no Repo is running.\n58\t    * AC4 enforcement: `Nebu.Web.LogStore.should_persist?/2`\n59\t      `(level, route_template)` — the route-aware policy check the ingest\n60\t      path needs. An off-list route with NO active capture is NEVER\n61\t      persisted (not even errors — AC4's literal \"entries for these\n62\t      endpoints are NOT persisted\", flagged in the handoff report); any\n63\t      other route (or a nil route) delegates to the existing level-only\n64\t      `/1` policy. The capture-active branch needs the DB and is pinned in\n65\t      `temporary_capture_manager_test.exs`.\n66\t    * AC12 (Option B — ANSWERED design question 2): the audit-action\n67\t      vocabulary `enable_temporary_capture` / `disable_temporary_capture`\n68\t      must be accepted by `Nebu.Compliance.AuditWriter.log/6`'s\n69\t      `@known_actions` allowlist (\"Adding a new audit action requires\n70\t      updating this list first\" — audit_writer.ex's own doc).\n71\t  \"\"\"\n72\t\n73\t  # async: false — the duration/audit describe blocks mutate process-global\n74\t  # Application env keys ({:nebu, :temporary_capture_duration_s} and\n75\t  # {:compliance, :repo}), same reasoning as audit_writer_test.exs.\n76\t  use ExUnit.Case, async: false\n77\t\n78\t  alias Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap\n79\t  alias Nebu.Admin.TemporaryCaptureManager\n80\t\n81\t  @off_defaults [\"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\", \"/admin/keepalive\", \"/health\"]\n82\t\n83\t  # default list is exactly sync, keepalive, and health\" do\n89\t      assert Enum.sort(TraceMap.long_polling_endpoints()) == Enum.sort(@off_defaults)\n90\t    end\n91\t\n92\t    test \"off_endpoint?/1 off-list and must resolve as off\"\n96\t      end\n97\t    end\n98\t\n99\t    test \"off_endpoint?/1 is false for ordinary endpoints, domains, and nebu\" do\n100\t      refute TraceMap.off_endpoint?(\"/admin/users\")\n101\t      refute TraceMap.off_endpoint?(\"/_matrix/client/v3/login\")\n102\t      refute TraceMap.off_endpoint?(\"/_matrix/client\")\n103\t      refute    @order [\n113\t      \"/admin/users\",\n114\t      \"/admin/rooms\",\n115\t      \"/_matrix/client/v3/login\",\n116\t      \"/_matrix/media/v3/upload\"\n117\t    ]\n118\t\n119\t    test \"a satellite key cascades only to itself ('Applies to this endpoint only')\" do\n120\t      assert TraceMap.cascade_targets(\"/admin/users\", @order) == [\"/admin/users\"]\n121\t    end\n122\t\n123\t [\"/admin/rooms\", \"/admin/users\"]\n126\t    end\n127\t\n128\t    test \"nebu cascades to every observed endpoint on the map\" do\n129\t      assert Enum.sort(TraceMap.cascade_targets(\"nebu\", key AND a satellite key\n134\t      # (TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint(\"/health\") == \"/health\") — the\n135\t      # cascade set must not contain it twice (the same dedupe class as\n136\t      # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n144\t\n145\t  describe \"AC5/AC6: TemporaryCaptureManager pure expiry comparison\" do\n146\t    @capture %{\n147\t      endpoint: \"/health\",\n148\t      enabled_at: ~U[2026-07-07 10:00:00Z],\n149\t      expires_at: ~U[2026-07-07 10:05:00Z],\n150\t      started_by: \"@admin:test.local\"\n151\t    }\n152\t\n153\t    test \"expired?/2 is false strictly before expires_at\" do\n154\t      refute TemporaryCaptureManager.expired?(@capture, ~U[2026-07-07 10:04:59Z])\n155\t    end\n156\t\n157\t    test \"expired?/2 is
