11:00:00Z])\n160\t    end\n161\t\n162\t    test \"remaining_seconds/2 counts down to the expiry\" do\n163\t      assert TemporaryCaptureManager.remaining_seconds(@capture, ~U[2026-07-07 10:03:30Z]) == 90\n164\t    end\n165\t\n166\t    test \"remaining_seconds/2 clamps at zero after expiry (never negative)\" do\n167\t      assert TemporaryCaptureManager.remaining_seconds(@capture, ~U[2026-07-07 10:06:00Z]) == 0\n168\t    end\n169\t  end\n170\t\n171\t  describe \"AC5: capture duration comes from Application env (war_room_manager precedent)\" do\n172\t    test \"capture_duration_s/0 defaults to 300 seconds (5 minutes)\" do\n173\t      Application.delete_env(:nebu, :temporary_capture_duration_s)\n174\t      assert TemporaryCaptureManager.capture_duration_s() == 300\n175\t    end\n176\t\n177\t    test \"capture_duration_s/0 honors the :temporary_capture_duration_s override\" do\n178\t      Application.put_env(:nebu, :temporary_capture_duration_s, 7)\n179\t      on_exit(fn -> Application.delete_env(:nebu, :temporary_capture_duration_s) end)\n180\t\n181\t      assert TemporaryCaptureManager.capture_duration_s() == 7\n182\t    end\n183\t  end\n184\t\n185\t  describe \"AC5: active?/1 is DB-guarded (unit-test contract, no Nebu.Repo running)\" do\n186\t    test \"with no Repo, no endpoint has an active capture\" do\n187\t      refute Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo),\n188\t             \"precondition: this is the DB-free unit environment\"\n189\t\n190\t      refute TemporaryCaptureManager.active?(\"/health\"),\n191\t             \"AC5: without a reachable captures table there is no active capture — \" <>\n192\t               \"mirror initial_war_room_status/0's Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) guard\"\n193\t    end\n194\t  end\n195\t\n196\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n197\t  # AC4: route-aware persistence policy (server-side enforcement)\n198\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n199\t\n200\t  describe \"AC4: should_persist?/2 blocks off-list routes with no active capture\" do\n201\t    test \"an off-list route is never persisted without a capture — not even errors\" do\n202\t      for route <- @off_defaults do\n203\t        refute Nebu.Web.LogStore.should_persist?(\"error\", route),\n204\t               \"AC4: entries for off-list route #{route} must NOT be persisted \" <>\n205\t                 \"(no active capture) regardless of level\"\n206\t\n207\t        refute Nebu.Web.LogStore.should_persist?(\"warning\", route)\n208\t      end\n209\t    end\n210\t\n211\t    test \"a non-off route delegates to the level-only policy (error/warning always persist)\" do\n212\t      assert Nebu.Web.LogStore.should_persist?(\"error\", \"/admin/users\")\n213\t      assert Nebu.Web.LogStore.should_persist?(\"warning\", \"/_matrix/client/v3/login\")\n214\t    end\n215\t\n216\t    test \"a nil route (no route_template) delegates to the level-only policy\" do\n217\t      assert Nebu.Web.LogStore.should_persist?(\"error\", nil)\n218\t    end\n219\t  end\n220\t\n221\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n222\t  # AC12 (Option B): audit-action vocabulary\n223\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n224\t\n225\t  describe \"AC12: enable/disable_temporary_capture are known audit actions\" do\n226\t    # FakeRepo pattern copied from audit_writer_test.exs — the allowlist\n227\t    # check happens BEFORE any DB call, so a fake repo isolates exactly the\n228\t    # vocabulary contract: today these two actions are rejected with\n229\t    # {:error, :audit_unknown_action} (red); once added to @known_actions\n230\t    # the write goes through the fake and returns :ok (green).\n231\t    defmodule FakeRepo do\n232\t      def transaction(fun) when is_function(fun, 0), do: {:ok, fun.()}\n233\t      def insert(struct, _opts \\\\ []), do: {:ok, struct}\n234\t\n235\t      def insert!(struct, opts \\\\ []) do\n236\t        {:ok, s} = insert(struct, opts)\n237\t        s\n238\t      end\n239\t    end\n240\t\n241\t    setup do\n242\t      Application.put_env(:compliance, :repo, FakeRepo)\n243\t      on_exit(fn -> Application.delete_env(:compliance, :repo) end)\n244\t      :ok\n245\t    end\n246\t\n247\t    test \"enable_temporary_capture is accepted by the AuditWriter allowlist\" do\n248\t      assert Nebu.Compliance.AuditWriter.log(\n249\t               \"@admin:test.local\",\n250\t               \"enable_temporary_capture\",\n251\t               \"endpoint\",\n252\t               \"/health\",\n253\t               %{},\n254\t               \"success\"\n255\t             ) == :ok\n256\t    end\n257\t\n258\t    test \"disable_temporary_capture is accepted by the AuditWriter allowlist\" do\n259\t      assert Nebu.Compliance.AuditWriter.log(\n260\t               \"@admin:test.local\",\n261\t               \"disable_temporary_capture\",\n262\t               \"endpoint\",\n263\t               \"/health\",\n264\t               %{},\n265\t               \"success\"\n266\t             ) == :ok\n267\t    end\n268\t  end\n269\tend\n270\t"}]},"uuid":"0d398085-fb1c-42b5-a6b5-4e8b83f3da22","timestamp":"2026-07-07T18:53:00.441Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/test/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_node_controls_contract_test.exs","content":"defmodule    test \"off_endpoint?/1 resolves membership for every default member\" do\n      for route <- @off_defaults do\n        assert TraceMap.off_endpoint?(route),\n               \"AC4: #{route} is in the default off-list and must resolve as off\"\n      end\n    end\n\n    test \"off_endpoint?/1 is false for ordinary endpoints, domains, and nebu\" do\n      refute TraceMap.off_endpoint?(\"/admin/users\")\n      refute TraceMap.off_endpoint?(\"/_matrix/client/v3/login\")\n      refute TraceMap.off_endpoint?(\"/_matrix/client\")\n      refute TraceMap.off_endpoint?(\"nebu\")\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # AC1/AC2: cascade-set computation (pure)\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"AC1/AC2: cascade_targets/2 computes the descendant set for a config change\" do\n    @order [\n      \"/admin/users\",\n      \"/admin/rooms\",\n      \"/_matrix/client/v3/login\",\n      \"/_matrix/media/v3/upload\"\n    ]\n\n    test \"a satellite key cascades only to itself ('Applies to this endpoint only')\" do\n      assert TraceMap.cascade_targets(\"/admin/users\", @order) == [\"/admin/users\"]\n    end\n\n    test \"a domain key cascades to every observed endpoint of that domain, and no others\" do\n      assert Enum.sort(TraceMap.cascade_targets(\"/admin\", @order)) ==\n               [\"/admin/rooms\", \"/admin/users\"]\n    end\n\n    test \"nebu cascades to every observed endpoint on the map\" do\n      assert Enum.sort(TraceMap.cascade_targets(\"nebu\", @order)) == Enum.sort(@order)\n    end\n\n    test \"a single-segment route that is its own domain cascades to itself exactly once\" do\n      # /health is simultaneously a domain key AND a satellite key\n      # (TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint(\"/health\") == \"/health\") — the\n      # cascade set must not contain it twice (the same dedupe class as\n      # code-review M1 in trace_map_leitstand_test.exs).\n      assert TraceMap.cascade_targets(\"/health\", [\"/health\" | @order]) == [\"/health\"]\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # AC5/AC6: capture expiry is a pure comparison; duration is env-config\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"AC5/AC6: TemporaryCaptureManager pure expiry comparison\" do\n    @capture %{\n      endpoint: \"/health\",\n      enabled_at: ~U[2026-07-07 10:00:00Z],\n      expires_at: ~U[2026-07-07 10:05:00Z],\n      started_by: \"@admin:test.local\"\n    }\n\n    test \"expired?/2 is false strictly before expires_at\" do\n      refute TemporaryCaptureManager.expired?(@capture, ~U[2026-07-07 10:04:59Z])\n    end\n\n    test \"expired?/2 is true at and after expires_at\" do\n      assert TemporaryCaptureManager.expired?(@capture, ~U[2026-07-07 10:05:00Z])\n      assert TemporaryCaptureManager.expired?(@capture, ~U[2026-07-07 11:00:00Z])\n    end\n\n    test \"remaining_seconds/2 counts down to the expiry\" do\n      assert TemporaryCaptureManager.remaining_seconds(@capture, ~U[2026-07-07 10:03:30Z]) == 90\n    end\n\n    test \"remaining_seconds/2 clamps at zero after expiry (never negative)\" do\n      assert TemporaryCaptureManager.remaining_seconds(@capture, ~U[2026-07-07 10:06:00Z]) == 0\n    end\n  end\n\n  describe \"AC5: capture duration comes from Application env (war_room_manager precedent)\" do\n    test \"capture_duration_s/0 defaults to 300 seconds (5 minutes)\" do\n      Application.delete_env(:nebu, :temporary_capture_duration_s)\n      assert TemporaryCaptureManager.capture_duration_s() == 300\n    end\n\n    test \"capture_duration_s/0 honors the :temporary_capture_duration_s override\" do\n      Application.put_env(:nebu, :temporary_capture_duration_s, 7)\n      on_exit(fn -> Application.delete_env(:nebu, :temporary_capture_duration_s) end)\n\n      assert TemporaryCaptureManager.capture_duration_s() == 7\n    end\n  end\n\n  describe \"AC5: active?/1 is DB-guarded (unit-test contract, no Nebu.Repo running)\" do\n    test \"with no Repo, no endpoint has an active capture\" do\n      refute Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo),\n             \"precondition: this is the DB-free unit environment\"\n\n      refute TemporaryCaptureManager.active?(\"/health\"),\n             \"AC5: without a reachable captures table there is no active capture — \" <>\n               \"mirror initial_war_room_status/0's Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) guard\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # AC4: route-aware persistence policy (server-side enforcement)\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"AC4: should_persist?/2 blocks off-list routes with no active capture\" do\n    test \"an off-list route is never persisted without a capture — not even errors\" do\n      for route <- @off_defaults do\n        refute Nebu.Web.LogStore.should_persist?(\"error\", route),\n               \"AC4: entries for off-list route #{route} must NOT be persisted \" <>\n                 \"(no active capture) regardless of level\"\n\n        refute Nebu.Web.LogStore.should_persist?(\"warning\", route)\n      end\n    end\n\n    test \"a non-off route delegates to the level-only policy (error/warning always persist)\" do\n      assert Nebu.Web.LogStore.should_persist?(\"error\", \"/admin/users\")\n      assert Nebu.Web.LogStore.should_persist?(\"warning\", \"/_matrix/client/v3/login\")\n    end\n\n    test \"a nil route (no route_template) delegates to the level-only policy\" do\n      assert Nebu.Web.LogStore.should_persist?(\"error\", nil)\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # AC12 (Option B): audit-action vocabulary\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"AC12: enable/disable_temporary_capture are known audit actions\" do\n    # FakeRepo pattern copied from audit_writer_test.exs — the allowlist\n    # check happens BEFORE any DB call, so a fake repo isolates exactly the\n    # vocabulary contract: today these two actions are rejected with\n    # {:error, :audit_unknown_action} (red); once added to @known_actions\n    # the write goes through the fake and returns :ok (green).\n    defmodule FakeRepo do\n      def transaction(fun) when is_function(fun, 0), do: {:ok, fun.()}\n      def insert(struct, _opts \\\\ []), do: {:ok, struct}\n\n      def insert!(struct, opts \\\\ []) do\n        {:ok, s} = insert(struct, opts)\n        s\n      end\n    end\n\n    setup do\n      Application.put_env(:compliance, :repo, FakeRepo)\n      on_exit(fn -> Application.delete_env(:compliance, :repo) end)\n      :ok\n    end\n\n    test \"enable_temporary_capture is accepted by the AuditWriter allowlist\" do\n      assert Nebu.Compliance.AuditWriter.log(\n               \"@admin:test.local\",\n               \"enable_temporary_capture\",\n               \"endpoint\",\n               \"/health\",\n               %{},\n               \"success\"\n             ) == :ok\n    end\n\n    test \"disable_temporary_capture is accepted by the AuditWriter allowlist\" do\n      assert Nebu.Compliance.AuditWriter.log(\n               \"@admin:test.local\",\n               \"disable_temporary_capture\",\n               \"endpoint\",\n               \"/health\",\n               %{},\n               \"success\"\n             ) == :ok\n    end\n  end\nend\n","numLines":270,"startLine":1,"totalLines":270}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"66b712b1-ecbf-451f-9df3-d47bc28b12e5","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

{"parentUuid":"d09b0b98-d243-4b77-a3b2-e68fcf70388d","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"ff672ad9-cc7f-4934-8af5-a1eaa250ea13","agentId":"a7b9f01d8dbd03b21","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01JE9VtoG4Ji7yk1CvLsd416","type":"tool_result","content":"1\tdefmodule Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapNodeControlsTest do\n2\t  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n3\t  RED-phase ATDD tests for `enhanced-logging/trace-map-node-controls` — the\n4\t  LiveView rendering/event half (AC1, AC2, AC3, AC4, AC5, AC6, AC7, AC8,\n5\t  AC9, AC10, AC12).\n6\t\n7\t  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/trace-map-node-controls.md\n8\t  Design: _bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/design/trace-map-leitstand-spec.md §4/§5\n9\t  Visual/copy authority: trace-map-leitstand-dummy.html (same directory) —\n10\t  \"the dummy IS the spec for visual treatment, copy, and interaction feel\".\n11\t\n12\t  Pure-logic contracts (off-list, cascade set, expiry comparison, duration\n13\t  env, should_persist?/2, audit vocabulary) live in\n14\t  `trace_map_node_controls_contract_test.exs`; the DB-backed\n15\t  `Nebu.Admin.TemporaryCaptureManager` API in\n16\t  `temporary_capture_manager_test.exs`; the browser-only half (REAL focus\n17\t  preservation across live patches, per-second countdown ticking, aria-live\n18\t  in a real AT tree) in `e2e/features/admin/logs-trace-map-node-controls.feature`\n19\t  (@pending-feature, Scenarios A–F per the story's AC11).\n20\t\n21\t  This file COMPOSES with the shipped `trace_map_leitstand_test.exs` — its\n22\t  helpers are mirrored, none of its pinned contracts are contradicted. All\n23\t  selectors on `/health` are KIND-scoped (`[data-node-kind=...]`): a\n24\t  single-segment route is simultaneously its own domain AND a satellite\n25\t  (accepted design, leitstand code-review i3), so a key-only selector\n26\t  matches two elements.\n27\t\n28\t  ## Why these MUST FAIL right now\n29\t\n30\t  The shipped Leitstand renders off-list endpoints like any other node\n31\t  (they only appear once traffic is observed, with numeric counts and\n32\t  ok/warn/error statuses), and the inspector has NO \"Logging\" section — no\n33\t  selects, no capture controls, no `configure_logging`/`enable_capture`/\n34\t  `disable_capture` events. Every test below red-fails on missing markup or\n35\t  missing event handlers — never on a compile error in this file.\n36\t\n37\t  ## Data-attribute / event contract this file pins down (extends, never\n38\t  ## replaces, the leitstand file's contract)\n39\t\n40\t    * Off-list endpoints (`TraceMap.long_polling_endpoints/0`) are ALWAYS\n41\t      on the map — rendered from mount, traffic or not (they collect no\n42\t      data, so waiting for traffic would never show them):\n43\t      `data-node-status=\"off\"`, node count text \"off\" (not a number), no\n44\t      warn/error badges, aria-label contains \"logging off\" (AC4/AC8/AC9).\n45\t    * Off propagation (AC4 / Known Challenge 1): a domain whose observed\n46\t      children are ALL off is itself `data-node-status=\"off\"` (at a clean\n47\t      mount that includes `/_matrix/client` — its only child is the off\n48\t      `/sync` — and `/admin` — only child `/admin/keepalive` — and the\n49\t      `/health` domain node). Once a NON-off child exists, the domain\n50\t      reflects its children's health again while off children stay off.\n51\t    * Off entries are NOT part of the leitstand's signal (AC4 \"the status\n52\t      window shows zero requests\"): a live PubSub broadcast for an off-list\n53\t      route with no active capture updates NOTHING — no node counts, no\n54\t      badges, no stream line, no nebu aggregation.\n55\t    * Inspector logging section (AC1/AC2, dummy copy):\n56\t      `[data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-logging\"]` with heading \"Logging\";\n57\t      `select[data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-level-select\"]` (options\n58\t      DEBUG/INFO/WARN/ERROR) and\n59\t      `select[data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-retention-select\"]` (option\n60\t      labels \"1 h\" / \"24 h\" / \"7 d\" / \"30 d\"), each wired to a `<label\n61\t      for=...>` (\"Min level\" / \"Retention\", AC9). Both live inside\n62\t      `form[data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-logging-form\"]` with\n63\t      `phx-change=\"configure_logging\"` (AC10: LiveView event, CSRF-safe by\n64\t      construction, no inline JS). The change params carry `node_key`,\n65\t      `min_level`, `retention_days` (Dev Notes' hidden-field variant).\n66\t    * Scope note `[data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-scope-note\"]` (AC1):\n67\t      satellite -> \"Applies to this endpoint only\"; domain -> \"Applies to\n68\t      all endpoints in this domain\"; nebu -> \"... on the map\". Clicking\n69\t      nebu therefore ALSO inspects it (it still clears filters — the\n70\t      leitstand contract is unchanged).\n71\t    * Capture row in the inspector for an inspected off satellite (dummy\n72\t      copy): `[data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-capture-state\"]` reading\n73\t      \"Logging off (long-polling)\" with an enable button\n74\t      `[data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-capture-toggle\"]`\n75\t      (`phx-click=\"enable_capture\"`); while capturing the state reads\n76\t      \"Capture active\" with countdown and the button becomes \"Disable now\"\n77\t      (`phx-click=\"disable_capture\"`, aria-label naming the endpoint —\n78\t      AC5/AC6/AC9).\n79\t    * Temp-capture node state (AC6/AC8): `data-node-status=\"temp\"`, a\n80\t      `[data-testid=\"trace-map-node-countdown\"]` text element in mm:ss\n81\t      carrying `data-expires-at` (unix seconds — the war-room banner's\n82\t      `data-started-at` precedent) so the TraceMapLeitstand hook can tick\n83\t      the countdown client-side with no server tick.\n84\t    * Interaction (AC5/AC7, the dummy's `activate()`): clicking an off,\n85\t      non-capturing satellite ENABLES capture (and inspects it) — it never\n86\t      toggles the stream filter; clicking it again WHILE capturing toggles\n87\t      the filter like any other node; \"Disable now\" ends the capture\n88\t      without clearing an active filter (Known Challenge 4).\n89\t    * Announcements (AC5/AC9) go through the existing\n90\t      `[data-testid=\"trace-map-announcer\"]` live region: enable ->\n91\t      \"Temporary capture enabled for <endpoint> · 5 minutes\"; disable ->\n92\t      \"Temporary capture disabled for <endpoint>\"; expiry -> \"Temporary\n93\t      capture expired for <endpoint>\" (asserted via =~ on the stable\n94\t      phrases, exact interpuncts not pinned).\n95\t\n96\t  ## Story-vs-dummy discrepancy, resolved in favor of the dummy + AC5/AC7\n97\t\n98\t  AC11's Scenario C prose shows a two-step enable (click node -> see an\n99\t  \"Enable capture\" button -> click it). The dummy's `activate()` and AC5's\n100\t  own headline (\"Clicking on an off-tier endpoint enables temporary\n101\t  capture\") + AC7 (\"a SECOND click ... toggles the filter\") pin a\n102\t  one-click enable. This file (and the E2E feature) follow the one-click\n103\t  contract; the inspector's enable button still exists (dummy line\n104\t  ~851: \"Enable for 5 min\") and is reachable after an early disable —\n105\t  flagged in the ATDD handoff report for the PM to confirm.\n106\t\n107\t  ## DB-free vs :integration split\n108\t\n109\t  Mirrors trace_map_leitstand_test.exs exactly: markup/event tests run\n110\t  DB-free under `make test-unit-elixir` (every capture/config DB touch in\n111\t  the implementation MUST be guarded behind `Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo)`);\n112\t  everything that genuinely persists (capture rows, server_config writes,\n113\t  cascade, pre-population, audit) is `@describetag :integration` and runs\n114\t  under `make test-integration` only.\n115\t  \"\"\"\n116\t\n117\t  use ExUnit.Case, async: false\n118\t\n119\t  import Plug.Test\n120\t  import Plug.Conn\n121\t  import Phoenix.LiveViewTest\n122\t  import Phoenix.ConnTest\n123\t\n124\t  @endpoint Nebu.Admin.Endpoint\n125\t  @secret_key_base String.duplicate(\"nebu-trace-map-node-controls-key\", 2)\n126\t  @topic \"operational_logs\"\n127\t\n128\t  @off_sync \"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\"\n129\t  @off_keepalive \"/admin/keepalive\"\n130\t  @off_health \"/health\"\n131\t\n132\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n133\t  # Helpers — mirror trace_map_leitstand_test.exs's admin_conn/mount pattern\n134\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n135\t\n136\t  defp admin_conn(path) do\n137\t    %{conn(:get, path) | secret_key_base: @secret_key_base}\n138\t    |> @endpoint.admin_session([])\n139\t    |> fetch_session()\n140\t    |> put_session(\"current_user\", %{\n141\t      \"admin\" => true,\n142\t      \"sub\" => \"admin-trace-map-node-controls-tests\",\n143\t      \"matrix_user_id\" => \"@admin:test.local\"\n144\t    })\n145\t  end\n146\t\n147\t  defp mount_trace_map(query_string \\\\ \"\") do\n148\t    path =\n149\t      if query_string == \"\",\n150\t        do: \"/admin/logs/trace-map\",\n151\t        else: \"/admin/logs/trace-map?\" <> query_string\n152\t\n153\t    sent_conn = path |> admin_conn() |> @endpoint.call(@endpoint.init([]))\n154\t    live(sent_conn)\n155\t  end\n156\t\n157\t  defp parsed(html), do: Floki.parse_document!(html)\n158\t\n159\t  defp find(html, selector), do: html |> Floki.find(selector) |> List.first()\n160\t  defp find_all(html, selector), do: Floki.find(html, selector)\n161\t  defp attr(node, name), do: node |> List.wrap() |> Floki.attribute(name) |> List.first()\n162\t\n163\t  # KIND-scoped node locator — mandatory for /health (domain AND satellite).\n164\t  defp node_locator(html, key, kind) do\n165\t    find(\n166\t      html,\n167\t      ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"][data-node-key=\"#{key}\"][data-node-kind=\"#{kind}\"])\n168\t    )\n169\t  end\n170\t\n171\t  defp node_selector(key, kind) do\n172\t    ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"][data-node-key=\"#{key}\"][data-node-kind=\"#{kind}\"])\n173\t  end\n174\t\n175\t  defp entry(overrides) do\n176\t    Map.merge(\n177\t      %{\n178\t        level: \"info\",\n179\t        source: \"http\",\n180\t        route_template: \"/foo\",\n181\t        request_id: \"req-\" <> Integer.to_string(System.unique_integer([:positive])),\n182\t        message: \"distinctive-node-controls-probe\",\n183\t        metadata: %{}\n184\t      },\n185\t      Map.new(overrides)\n186\t    )\n187\t  end\n188\t\n189\t  defp broadcast_entry(overrides) do\n190\t    e = entry(overrides)\n191\t    Phoenix.PubSub.broadcast(Nebu.PubSub, @topic, e)\n192\t    e\n193\t  end\n194\t\n195\t  defp click_node(view, key, kind) do\n196\t    view |> element(node_selector(key, kind)) |> render_click()\n197\t  end\n198\t\n199\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n200\t  # AC4/AC8: off-by-default rendering — always on the map, gray/dashed/\"off\"\n201\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n202\t\n203\t  describe \"AC4: the off-list endpoints are on the map from mount, with no traffic\" do\n204\t    test \"all three default off endpoints render as satellite nodes with status off\" do\n205\t      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_trace_map()\n206\t      doc = parsed(html)\n207\t\n208\t      for route <- [@off_sync, @off_keepalive, @off_health] do\n209\t        node = node_locator(doc, route, \"satellite\")\n210\t\n211\t        assert node,\n212\t               \"AC4: off-list endpoint #{route} must be rendered WITHOUT traffic \" <>\n213\t                 \"(it collects no data, so it can never appear by observation)\"\n214\t\n215\t        assert attr(node, \"data-node-status\") == \"off\",\n216\t               \"AC4: #{route} must render in the off state\"\n217\t      end\n218\t    end\n219\t\n220\t    test \"an off node's count reads the text \\\"off\\\", not a number, and carries no badges\" do\n221\t      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_trace_map()\n222\t      doc = parsed(html)\n223\t\n224\t      node = node_locator(doc, @off_health, \"satellite\")\n225\t      assert Floki.text(node) =~ \"off\", \"AC4: the node count must show the text \\\"off\\\"\"\n226\t\n227\t      refute find(node, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-badge-warn\"]))\n228\t      refute find(node, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-badge-error\"]))\n229\t    end\n230\t\n231\t    test \"off nodes' aria-labels read \\\"logging off\\\" (AC9, never color-alone)\" do\n232\t      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_trace_map()\n233\t      doc = parsed(html)\n234\t\n235\t      for route <- [@off_sync, @off_keepalive, @off_health] do\n236\t        label = attr(node_locator(doc, route, \"satellite\"), \"aria-label\")\n237\t\n238\t        assert label =~ route\n239\t        assert label =~ ~r/logging off/i,\n240\t               \"AC9: #{route}'s aria-label must state the off state in text\"\n241\t      end\n242\t    end\n243\t\n244\t    test \"a domain whose children are ALL off is itself off (Known Challenge 1)\" do\n245\t      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_trace_map()\n246\t      doc = parsed(html)\n247\t\n248\t      # At a clean mount each of these domains exists solely because of its\n249\t      # off child: /_matrix/client <- /sync, /admin <- /admin/keepalive,\n250\t      # /health <- itself.\n251\t      assert attr(node_locator(doc, \"/_matrix/client\", \"domain\"), \"data-node-status\") == \"off\"\n252\t      assert attr(node_locator(doc, \"/admin\", \"domain\"), \"data-node-status\") == \"off\"\n253\t      assert attr(node_locator(doc, @off_health, \"domain\"), \"data-node-status\") == \"off\"\n254\t    end\n255\t\n256\t    test \"a non-off sibling brings the domain back to health-status while the off child stays off\" do\n257\t      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n258\t      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/_matrix/client/v3/login\", level: \"info\")\n259\t\n260\t      doc = render(view) |> parsed()\n261\t\n262\t      assert attr(node_locator(doc, \"/_matrix/client/v3/login\", \"satellite\"), \"data-node-status\") ==\n263\t               \"ok\"\n264\t\n265\t      assert attr(node_locator(doc, \"/_matrix/client\", \"domain\"), \"data-node-status\") == \"ok\",\n266\t             \"AC4: with one live child, the domain reflects that child's health again\"\n267\t\n268\t      assert attr(node_locator(doc, @off_sync, \"satellite\"), \"data-node-status\") == \"off\",\n269\t             \"AC4: the off sibling must stay off\"\n270\t    end\n271\t  end\n272\t\n273\t  describe \"AC4: broadcasts for off-list endpoints (no capture) are not part of the signal\" do\n274\t    test \"a warning broadcast for an off endpoint changes neither its node nor its domain\" do\n275\t      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n276\t      broadcast_entry(route_template: @off_keepalive, level: \"warning\")\n277\t\n278\t      doc = render(view) |> never leaves the off state\"\n283\t\n284\t      refute find(node, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-badge-warn\"]))\n285\t      assert Floki.text(node) =~ \"off\"\n286\t\n287\t      assert attr(node_locator(doc, \"/admin\", \"domain\"), \"data-node-status\") == \"off\",\n288\t             \"AC4: no status propagation from an uncaptured off endpoint\"\n289\t    end\n290\t\n291\t    test \"an off endpoint's broadcast produces no stream line and no nebu aggregation\" do\n292\t      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n293\t\n294\t      broadcast_entry(\n295\t        route_template: @off_health,\n296\t        level: \"error\",\n297\t        message: \"distinctive-off-endpoint-line\"\n298\t      )\n299\t\n300\t      doc = render(view) |> parsed()\n301\t\n302\t      texts =\n303\t        doc\n304\t        |> find_all(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-stream-line\"]))\n305\t        |> Enum.map(&Floki.text/1)\n306\t\n307\t      refute Enum.any?(texts, &(&1 =~ \"distinctive-off-endpoint-line\")),\n308\t             \"AC4: uncollected off-endpoint entries must not appear in the stream panel\"\n309\t\n310\t      nebu_label = attr(node_locator(doc, \"nebu\", aria-label #{inspect(nebu_label)})\"\n315\t\n316\t      assert nebu_label =~ \"0 errors\"\n317\t    end\n318\t  end\n319\t\n320\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n321\t  # AC1/AC2/AC9: inspector logging controls markup\n322\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n323\t\n324\t  describe \"AC1/AC2: the inspected node's Logging section renders labelled selects\" do\n325\t    test \"clicking a satellite shows the Logging section with min-level and retention selects\" do\n326\t      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n327\t      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"info\")\n328\t      render(view)\n329\t\n330\t      html = click_node(view, \"/admin/users\", \"satellite\") |> parsed()\n331\t\n332\t      section = find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-logging\"]))\n333\t      assert section, \"AC1: expected a Logging section in the inspector\"\n334\t      assert Floki.text(section) =~ \"Logging\"\n335\t\n336\t      level_select = find(section, ~s(select[data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-level-select\"]))\n337\t      assert level_select, \"AC1: expected the min-level select\"\n338\t\n339\t      level_options = level_select |> Floki.find(\"option\") |> Enum.map(&String.trim(Floki.text(&1)))\n340\t      assert level_options == [\"DEBUG\", \"INFO\", \"WARN\", \"ERROR\"]\n341\t\n342\t      retention_select =\n343\t        find(section, ~s(select[data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-retention-select\"]))\n344\t\n345\t      assert retention_select, \"AC2: expected the retention select\"\n346\t\n347\t      retention_options =\n348\t        retention_select |> Floki.find(\"option\") |> Enum.map(&String.trim(Floki.text(&1)))\n349\t\n350\t      assert retention_options == [\"1 h\", \"24 h\", \"7 d\", \"30 d\"]\n351\t    end\n352\t\n353\t    test \"both selects are wired to <label for=...> elements (AC9)\" do\n354\t      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n355\t      broadcast_entry(route_template: <- [\n361\t            {\"trace-map-inspector-level-select\", ~r/min level/i},\n362\t            {\"trace-map-inspector-retention-select\", ~r/retention/i}\n363\t          ] do\n364\t        select = find(html, ~s(select[data-testid=\"#{select_testid}\"]))\n365\t        select_id = attr(select, \"id\")\n366\t\n367\t        assert select_id not in [nil, \"\"],\n368\t               \"AC9: #{select_testid} needs an id a <label for> can target\"\n369\t\n370\t        label = find(html, ~s(label[for=\"#{select_id}\"]))\n371\t        assert label, \"AC9: expected a <label for=\\\"#{select_id}\\\">\"\n372\t        assert Floki.text(label) =~ label_text\n373\t      end\n374\t    end\n375\t\n376\t    test \"the controls live in a form with phx-change=configure_logging
