Nebu.Admin.TemporaryCaptureManagerTest do\n2\t  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n3\t  RED-phase ATDD tests for `enhanced-logging/trace-map-node-controls` — the\n4\t  DB-backed `Nebu.Admin.TemporaryCaptureManager` public API (AC4, AC5,\n5\t  AC12), the cluster-safe capture timer of the story's ANSWERED design\n6\t  question 1: \"state is a row, expiry is a comparison against now() — no\n7\t  process timer owns correctness\" (the `Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager`\n8\t  precedent, ADR-018/020: a pure-functional, inline-SQL Admin-domain\n9\t  module).\n10\t\n11\t  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/trace-map-node-controls.md\n12\t\n13\t  Pure comparisons (`expired?/2`, `remaining_seconds/2`,\n14\t  `capture_duration_s/0`, the DB-free `active?/1` guard) are pinned in\n15\t  `logs_live/trace_map_node_controls_contract_test.exs`; this file is the\n16\t  genuinely-DB half and is `:integration`-tagged throughout (runs under\n17\t  `make test-integration` only, mirroring war_room_manager's own tests).\n18\t\n19\t  ## Why these MUST FAIL right now\n20\t\n21\t  Neither `Nebu.Admin.TemporaryCaptureManager` nor the\n22\t  `admin_temporary_captures` table (expected migration:\n23\t  `server/priv/migrations/000020_admin_temporary_captures.{up,down}.sql`,\n24\t  next sequential number after 000019_war_room_sessions) exists yet.\n25\t\n26\t  ## API contract pinned here\n27\t\n28\t    * `enable(endpoint, started_by)` -> `{:ok, capture}` — inserts the row\n29\t      (`endpoint` is the primary key: at most one active capture per\n30\t      endpoint, story Dev Notes schema) with `expires_at = enabled_at +\n31\t      capture_duration_s()` and returns the capture map\n32\t      (`%{endpoint:, enabled_at:, expires_at:, started_by:}`).\n33\t    * `disable(endpoint)` -> `:ok` (capture ended) or\n34\t      `{:error, :not_found}` (no active capture — a safe no-op error,\n35\t      never a crash; the war_room stop_session/1 shape).\n36\t    * `active?(endpoint)` -> whether a NON-EXPIRED row exists; an expired\n37\t      row still sitting in the table (cleanup hasn't run yet) counts as\n38\t      inactive — expiry is the `expires_at > now()` comparison, NOT the\n39\t      row's presence.\n40\t    * `active_captures/0` -> all non-expired captures.\n41\t    * `cleanup_expired/0` -> deletes expired rows, leaves active ones (the\n42\t      periodic sweeper's work function — story Dev Notes item 3).\n43\t    * AC12 Option B (ANSWERED design question 2): `enable/2` and\n44\t      `disable/1` write compliance audit entries via\n45\t      `Nebu.Compliance.AuditWriter` — `action:\n46\t      \"enable_temporary_capture\"` / `\"disable_temporary_capture\"`,\n47\t      `target_type: \"endpoint\"`, `target_id: <endpoint>`, actor = the\n48\t      admin who acted (retention-settings' audit precedent).\n49\t    * AC4: `Nebu.Web.LogStore.should_persist?/2` flips to the level-only\n50\t      policy for an off-list route WHILE its capture is active, and back\n51\t      to `false` after disable.\n52\t  \"\"\"\n53\t\n54\t  use ExUnit.Case, async: false\n55\t\n56\t  @moduletag :integration\n57\t\n58\t  alias Nebu.Admin.TemporaryCaptureManager\n59\t\n60\t  @endpoint_route \"/health\"\n61\t\n62\t  setup do\n63\t    Nebu.Release.migrate()\n64\t\n65\t    Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(Nebu.Repo, \"DELETE FROM admin_temporary_captures\", [])\n66\t\n67\t    Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n68\t      Nebu.Repo,\n69\t      \"DELETE FROM audit_log WHERE action IN ('enable_temporary_capture', 'disable_temporary_capture')\",\n70\t      []\n71\t    )\n72\t\n73\t    :ok\n74\t  end\n75\t\n76\t  defp insert_capture(endpoint, enabled_offset_sql, expires_offset_sql, started_by) do\n77\t    Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n78\t      Nebu.Repo,\n79\t      \"INSERT INTO admin_temporary_captures (endpoint, enabled_at, expires_at, started_by) \" <>\n80\t        \"VALUES ($1, now() #{enabled_offset_sql}, now() #{expires_offset_sql}, $2)\",\n81\t      [endpoint, started_by]\n82\t    )\n83\t  end\n84\t\n85\t  defp capture_rows(endpoint) do\n86\t    Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n87\t      Nebu.Repo,\n88\t      \"SELECT endpoint, started_by FROM admin_temporary_captures WHERE endpoint = $1\",\n89\t      [endpoint]\n90\t    ).rows\n91\t  end\n92\t\n93\t  defp audit_rows(action) do\n94\t    Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n95\t      Nebu.Repo,\n96\t      \"SELECT actor_user_id, target_type, target_id FROM audit_log WHERE action = $1\",\n97\t      [action]\n98\t    ).rows\n99\t  end\n100\t\n101\t  describe \"AC5: enable/2 — the DB row is the timer\" do\n102\t    test \"inserts one row with expires_at = enabled_at + configured duration\" do\n103\t      Application.put_env(:nebu, :temporary_capture_duration_s, 60)\n104\t      on_exit(fn -> Application.delete_env(:nebu, :temporary_capture_duration_s) end)\n105\t\n106\t      assert {:ok, capture} = TemporaryCaptureManager.enable(@endpoint_route, \"@admin:test.local\")\n107\t\n108\t      assert capture.endpoint == @endpoint_route\n109\t      assert capture.started_by == \"@admin:test.local\"\n110\t      assert %DateTime{} = capture.enabled_at\n111\t      assert %DateTime{} = capture.expires_at\n112\t\n113\t      assert_in_delta DateTime.diff(capture.expires_at, capture.enabled_at, :second), 60, 2\n114\t\n115\t      assert [[@endpoint_route, \"@admin:test.local\"]] = capture_rows(@endpoint_route)\n116\t    end\n117\t\n118\t    test \"active?/1 and active_captures/0 see the enabled capture\" do\n119\t      {:ok, _capture} = TemporaryCaptureManager.enable(@endpoint_route, \"@admin:test.local\")\n120\t\n121\t      assert TemporaryCaptureManager.active?(@endpoint_route)\n122\t      refute TemporaryCaptureManager.active?(\"/admin/keepalive\")\n123\t\n124\t      actives = TemporaryCaptureManager.active_captures()\n125\t      assert Enum.any?(actives, &(&1.endpoint == @endpoint_route))\n126\t    end\n127\t\n128\t    test \"an expired row is NOT active — presence is not the state, the comparison is\" do\n129\t      insert_capture(@endpoint_route, \"- interval '10 minutes'\", \"- interval '5 minutes'\", \"stale\")\n130\t\n131\t      refute TemporaryCaptureManager.active?(@endpoint_route),\n132\t             \"AC5: an expired row (cleanup pending) must never count as an active capture\"\n133\t\n134\t      assert TemporaryCaptureManager.active_captures() == []\n135\t    end\n136\t  end\n137\t\n138\t  describe \"AC5: disable/1 — early end\" do\n139\t    test \"disable ends an active capture; a second disable is {:error, :not_found}\" do\n140\t      {:ok, _capture} = TemporaryCaptureManager.enable(@endpoint_route, \"@admin:test.local\")\n141\t\n142\t      assert :ok = TemporaryCaptureManager.disable(@endpoint_route)\n143\t      refute TemporaryCaptureManager.active?(@endpoint_route)\n144\t\n145\t      assert {:error, :not_found} = TemporaryCaptureManager.disable(@endpoint_route)\n146\t    end\n147\t  end\n148\t\n149\t  describe \"AC5: cleanup_expired/0 — the periodic sweeper's work function\" do\n150\t    test \"deletes expired rows and leaves active ones untouched\" do\n151\t      insert_capture(\"/admin/keepalive\", \"- interval '10 minutes'\", \"- interval '5 minutes'\", \"stale\")\n152\t      insert_capture(@endpoint_route, \"\", \"+ interval '1 hour'\", \"fresh\")\n153\t\n154\t      TemporaryCaptureManager.cleanup_expired()\n155\t\n156\t      assert capture_rows(\"/admin/keepalive\") == [],\n157\t             \"the expired row must be swept\"\n158\t\n159\t      assert [[@endpoint_route, \"fresh\"]] = capture_rows(@endpoint_route),\n160\t             \"the active row must survive the sweep\"\n161\t    end\n162\t  end\n163\t\n164\t  describe \"AC12 (Option B): enable/disable write compliance audit entries\" do\n165\t    test \"enable/2 records an enable_temporary_capture audit row naming actor and endpoint\" do\n166\t      {:ok, _capture} = TemporaryCaptureManager.enable(@endpoint_route, \"@admin:test.local\")\n167\t\n168\t      assert [[\"@admin:test.local\", \"endpoint\", @endpoint_route]] =\n169\t               audit_rows(\"enable_temporary_capture\"),\n170\t             \"AC12: enabling collection on an off endpoint is an admin-initiated \" <>\n171\t               \"logging-policy change and must be audit-logged (retention-settings precedent)\"\n172\t    end\n173\t\n174\t    test \"disable/1 records a disable_temporary_capture audit row\" do\n175\t      {:ok, _capture} = TemporaryCaptureManager.enable(@endpoint_route, \"@admin:test.local\")\n176\t      :ok = TemporaryCaptureManager.disable(@endpoint_route)\n177\t\n178\t      assert [[_actor, \"endpoint\", @endpoint_route]] = audit_rows(\"disable_temporary_capture\")\n179\t    end\n180\t  end\n181\t\n182\t  describe \"AC4: should_persist?/2 flips with the capture lifecycle\" do\n183\t    test \"an off-list route persists (per level policy) exactly while its capture is active\" do\n184\t      refute Nebu.Web.LogStore.should_persist?(\"error\", @endpoint_route),\n185\t             \"precondition: off and uncaptured -> never persisted\"\n186\t\n187\t      {:ok, _capture} = TemporaryCaptureManager.enable(@endpoint_route, \"@admin:test.local\")\n188\t\n189\t      assert Nebu.Web.LogStore.should_persist?(\"error\", @endpoint_route),\n190\t             \"AC5: while capture is active, the policy check skips the off-list\"\n191\t\n192\t      :ok = TemporaryCaptureManager.disable(@endpoint_route)\n193\t\n194\t      refute Nebu.Web.LogStore.should_persist?(\"error\", @endpoint_route),\n195\t             \"AC5: after disable the endpoint reverts to off-tier enforcement\"\n196\t    end\n197\t  end\n198\tend\n199\t"}]},"uuid":"b8e797fb-a00c-485d-bca5-56d222bf5baa","timestamp":"2026-07-07T18:53:18.604Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/test/nebu/admin/temporary_capture_manager_test.exs","content":"defmodule Nebu.Admin.TemporaryCaptureManagerTest do\n  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n  RED-phase ATDD tests for `enhanced-logging/trace-map-node-controls` — the\n  DB-backed `Nebu.Admin.TemporaryCaptureManager` public API (AC4, AC5,\n  AC12), the cluster-safe capture timer of the story's ANSWERED design\n  question 1: \"state is a row, expiry is a comparison against now() — no\n  process timer owns correctness\" (the `Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager`\n  precedent, ADR-018/020: a pure-functional, inline-SQL Admin-domain\n  module).\n\n  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/trace-map-node-controls.md\n\n  Pure comparisons (`expired?/2`, `remaining_seconds/2`,\n  `capture_duration_s/0`, the DB-free `active?/1` guard) are pinned in\n  `logs_live/trace_map_node_controls_contract_test.exs`; this file is the\n  genuinely-DB half and is `:integration`-tagged throughout (runs under\n  `make test-integration` only, mirroring war_room_manager's own tests).\n\n  ## Why these MUST FAIL right now\n\n  Neither `Nebu.Admin.TemporaryCaptureManager` nor the\n  `admin_temporary_captures` table (expected migration:\n  `server/priv/migrations/000020_admin_temporary_captures.{up,down}.sql`,\n  next sequential number after 000019_war_room_sessions) exists yet.\n\n  ## API contract pinned here\n\n    * `enable(endpoint, started_by)` -> `{:ok, capture}` — inserts the row\n      (`endpoint` is the primary key: at most one active capture per\n      endpoint, story Dev Notes schema) with `expires_at = enabled_at +\n      capture_duration_s()` and returns the capture map\n      (`%{endpoint:, enabled_at:, expires_at:, started_by:}`).\n    * `disable(endpoint)` -> `:ok` (capture ended) or\n      `{:error, :not_found}` (no active capture — a safe no-op error,\n      never a crash; the war_room stop_session/1 shape).\n    * `active?(endpoint)` -> whether a NON-EXPIRED row exists; an expired\n      row still sitting in the table (cleanup hasn't run yet) counts as\n      inactive — expiry is the `expires_at > now()` comparison, NOT the\n      row's presence.\n    * `active_captures/0` -> all non-expired captures.\n    * `cleanup_expired/0` -> deletes expired rows, leaves active ones (the\n      periodic sweeper's work function — story Dev Notes item 3).\n    * AC12 Option B (ANSWERED design question 2): `enable/2` and\n      `disable/1` write compliance audit entries via\n      `Nebu.Compliance.AuditWriter` — `action:\n      \"enable_temporary_capture\"` / `\"disable_temporary_capture\"`,\n      `target_type: \"endpoint\"`, `target_id: <endpoint>`, actor = the\n      admin who acted (retention-settings' audit precedent).\n    * AC4: `Nebu.Web.LogStore.should_persist?/2` flips to the level-only\n      policy for an off-list route WHILE its capture is active, and back\n      to `false` after disable.\n  \"\"\"\n\n  use ExUnit.Case, async: false\n\n  @moduletag :integration\n\n  alias Nebu.Admin.TemporaryCaptureManager\n\n  @endpoint_route \"/health\"\n\n  setup do\n    Nebu.Release.migrate()\n\n    Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(Nebu.Repo, \"DELETE FROM admin_temporary_captures\", [])\n\n    Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n      Nebu.Repo,\n      \"DELETE FROM audit_log WHERE action IN ('enable_temporary_capture', 'disable_temporary_capture')\",\n      []\n    )\n\n    :ok\n  end\n\n  defp insert_capture(endpoint, enabled_offset_sql, expires_offset_sql, started_by) do\n    Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n      Nebu.Repo,\n      \"INSERT INTO admin_temporary_captures (endpoint, enabled_at, expires_at, started_by) \" <>\n        \"VALUES ($1, now() #{enabled_offset_sql}, now() #{expires_offset_sql}, $2)\",\n      [endpoint, started_by]\n    )\n  end\n\n  defp capture_rows(endpoint) do\n    Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n      Nebu.Repo,\n      \"SELECT endpoint, started_by FROM admin_temporary_captures WHERE endpoint = $1\",\n      [endpoint]\n    ).rows\n  end\n\n  defp audit_rows(action) do\n    Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n      Nebu.Repo,\n      \"SELECT actor_user_id, target_type, target_id FROM audit_log WHERE action = $1\",\n      [action]\n    ).rows\n  end\n\n  describe \"AC5: enable/2 — the DB row is the timer\" do\n    test \"inserts one row with expires_at = enabled_at + configured duration\" do\n      Application.put_env(:nebu, :temporary_capture_duration_s, 60)\n      on_exit(fn -> Application.delete_env(:nebu, :temporary_capture_duration_s) end)\n\n      assert {:ok, capture} = TemporaryCaptureManager.enable(@endpoint_route, \"@admin:test.local\")\n\n      assert capture.endpoint == @endpoint_route\n      assert capture.started_by == \"@admin:test.local\"\n      assert %DateTime{} = capture.enabled_at\n      assert %DateTime{} = capture.expires_at\n\n      assert_in_delta DateTime.diff(capture.expires_at, capture.enabled_at, :second), 60, 2\n\n      assert [[@endpoint_route, \"@admin:test.local\"]] = capture_rows(@endpoint_route)\n    end\n\n    test \"active?/1 and active_captures/0 see the enabled capture\" do\n      {:ok, _capture} = TemporaryCaptureManager.enable(@endpoint_route, \"@admin:test.local\")\n\n      assert TemporaryCaptureManager.active?(@endpoint_route)\n      refute TemporaryCaptureManager.active?(\"/admin/keepalive\")\n\n      actives = TemporaryCaptureManager.active_captures()\n      assert Enum.any?(actives, &(&1.endpoint == @endpoint_route))\n    end\n\n    test \"an expired row is NOT active — presence is not the state, the comparison is\" do\n      insert_capture(@endpoint_route, \"- interval '10 minutes'\", \"- interval '5 minutes'\", \"stale\")\n\n      refute TemporaryCaptureManager.active?(@endpoint_route),\n             \"AC5: an expired row (cleanup pending) must never count as an active capture\"\n\n      assert TemporaryCaptureManager.active_captures() == []\n    end\n  end\n\n  describe \"AC5: disable/1 — early end\" do\n    test \"disable ends an active capture; a second disable is {:error, :not_found}\" do\n      {:ok, _capture} = TemporaryCaptureManager.enable(@endpoint_route, \"@admin:test.local\")\n\n      assert :ok = TemporaryCaptureManager.disable(@endpoint_route)\n      refute TemporaryCaptureManager.active?(@endpoint_route)\n\n      assert {:error, :not_found} = TemporaryCaptureManager.disable(@endpoint_route)\n    end\n  end\n\n  describe \"AC5: cleanup_expired/0 — the periodic sweeper's work function\" do\n    test \"deletes expired rows and leaves active ones untouched\" do\n      insert_capture(\"/admin/keepalive\", \"- interval '10 minutes'\", \"- interval '5 minutes'\", \"stale\")\n      insert_capture(@endpoint_route, \"\", \"+ interval '1 hour'\", \"fresh\")\n\n      TemporaryCaptureManager.cleanup_expired()\n\n      assert capture_rows(\"/admin/keepalive\") == [],\n             \"the expired row must be swept\"\n\n      assert [[@endpoint_route, \"fresh\"]] = capture_rows(@endpoint_route),\n             \"the active row must survive the sweep\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  describe \"AC12 (Option B): enable/disable write compliance audit entries\" do\n    test \"enable/2 records an enable_temporary_capture audit row naming actor and endpoint\" do\n      {:ok, _capture} = TemporaryCaptureManager.enable(@endpoint_route, \"@admin:test.local\")\n\n      assert [[\"@admin:test.local\", \"endpoint\", @endpoint_route]] =\n               audit_rows(\"enable_temporary_capture\"),\n             \"AC12: enabling collection on an off endpoint is an admin-initiated \" <>\n               \"logging-policy change and must be audit-logged (retention-settings precedent)\"\n    end\n\n    test \"disable/1 records a disable_temporary_capture audit row\" do\n      {:ok, _capture} = TemporaryCaptureManager.enable(@endpoint_route, \"@admin:test.local\")\n      :ok = TemporaryCaptureManager.disable(@endpoint_route)\n\n      assert [[_actor, \"endpoint\", @endpoint_route]] = audit_rows(\"disable_temporary_capture\")\n    end\n  end\n\n  describe \"AC4: should_persist?/2 flips with the capture lifecycle\" do\n    test \"an off-list route persists (per level policy) exactly while its capture is active\" do\n      refute Nebu.Web.LogStore.should_persist?(\"error\", @endpoint_route),\n             \"precondition: off and uncaptured -> never persisted\"\n\n      {:ok, _capture} = TemporaryCaptureManager.enable(@endpoint_route, \"@admin:test.local\")\n\n      assert Nebu.Web.LogStore.should_persist?(\"error\", @endpoint_route),\n             \"AC5: while capture is active, the policy check skips the off-list\"\n\n      :ok = TemporaryCaptureManager.disable(@endpoint_route)\n\n      refute Nebu.Web.LogStore.should_persist?(\"error\", @endpoint_route),\n             \"AC5: after disable the endpoint reverts to off-tier enforcement\"\n    end\n  end\nend\n","numLines":199,"startLine":1,"totalLines":199}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"382ee62a-8e7c-4c66-9852-c0be5e9c0f3e","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

{"parentUuid":"843d608b-1d21-4142-9cca-30b3d40c79ae","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"ff672ad9-cc7f-4934-8af5-a1eaa250ea13","agentId":"a7b9f01d8dbd03b21","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01YAGSvS9kho8zF88QEKHacT","type":"tool_result","content":".../admin/logs-trace-map-node-controls.feature     | 120 +++++++++++++++++++++\n 1 file changed, 120 insertions(+)\n\n--- Changes ---\n\ne2e/features/admin/logs-trace-map-node-controls.feature\n  @@ -0,0 +1,120 @@\n  +Feature: Admin UI — Trace Map node controls (inspector logging panel + temporary capture)\n  +  As a Nebu platform operator managing production logs\n  +  I want the Trace Map inspector to configure per-endpoint collection levels and retention,\n  +    and long-polling endpoints to be off by default with a one-click 5-minute temporary capture\n  +  So that I can tune collection policy from the map, safely experiment with high-frequency\n  +    endpoints, and keep the map's signal-to-noise ratio high\n  +\n  +  # Story enhanced-logging/trace-map-node-controls — ATDD, written FIRST (RED phase).\n  +  # docs/stories/enhanced-logging/trace-map-node-controls.md\n  +  # Design: _bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/design/trace-map-leitstand-spec.md §4/§5\n  +  # Interactive reference: trace-map-leitstand-dummy.html (same directory).\n  +  #\n  +  # Every scenario is tagged @pending-feature (excluded from the default run\n  +  # by playwright.config.ts's DEFAULT_E2E_TAGS) until the dev-story lands —\n  +  # the established un-tagging convention (logs-trace-map.feature's history).\n  +  #\n  +  # These are the story's AC11 Scenarios A–F. Server-side halves live in:\n  +  #   - server/test/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_node_controls_contract_test.exs\n  +  #     (off-list, cascade set, expiry comparison, audit vocabulary)\n  +  #   - server/test/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_node_controls_test.exs\n  +  #     (inspector markup/events, off rendering, capture lifecycle)\n  +  #   - server/test/nebu/admin/temporary_capture_manager_test.exs (DB-backed timer API)\n  +  #\n  +  # INTERACTION NOTE (story-vs-dummy discrepancy, resolved for the dummy +\n  +  # AC5/AC7): AC11's Scenario C prose sketches a two-step enable (click node\n  +  # -> see an \"Enable capture\" button -> click it). The approved dummy's\n  +  # `activate()` and AC5's own headline pin a ONE-CLICK enable on an off\n  +  # node (a SECOND click while capturing toggles the filter, AC7). Scenario\n  +  # C below follows the one-click contract; the inspector's own\n  +  # \"Enable for 5 min\" button is exercised in Scenario E after the early\n  +  # disable. Flagged in the ATDD handoff report for the PM to confirm.\n  +  #\n  +  # TIMER SEEDING (ANSWERED design question 3): scenarios that must observe\n  +  # expiry seed an about-to-expire admin_temporary_captures row via\n  +  # e2e/fixtures/db.ts (insertTemporaryCapture) — never a 5-minute wait, and\n  +  # NO test-only HTTP routes. Countdown ticking is awaited via\n  +  # waitForFunction on the countdown text, never bare sleeps.\n  +  #\n  +  # /health SELECTOR NOTE: a single-segment route is simultaneously its own\n  +  # domain AND a satellite on the map (accepted leitstand design) — every\n  +  # /health step here uses the KIND-scoped \"satellite node\" steps to avoid\n  +  # Playwright strict-mode violations.\n  +\n @node-controls-config\n  +  Scenario: A — Inspector loads with the endpoint's current logging config and keeps focus\n  +    Given a request to \"/admin/users\" has been recorded\n  +    And the logging config for \"/admin/users\" is min level \"ERROR\" with retention days \"30\"\n  +    When the operator navigates to \"/admin/logs/trace-map\"\n  +    And the operator clicks the \"/admin/users\" node on the Trace Map\n  +    Then the inspector logging panel shows min level \"ERROR\" and retention \"30 d\"\n  +    And the inspector scope note reads \"Applies to this endpoint Same approved direct-SQL mechanism as above (NO\n  +// test-only HTTP routes, ever). The story's ANSWERED design question 3\n  +// explicitly blesses \"seed an about-to-expire capture row via the db.ts\n  +// fixture\" as the E2E alternative to waiting out the 5-minute timer.\n  +// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  +\n  +/**\n  + * Run one SQL query and return its raw output (`-t -A`: tuples only,\n  + * unaligned) — the read-side sibling of runSql for scenario assertions that\n  + * verify persistence \"via a Playwright DB query\" (story AC11, Scenario B).\n  + */\n  +function runSqlQuery(sql: string): string {\n  +  const cmd = DB_URL\n  +    ? `psql \"${DB_URL}\" -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -t -A -c ${JSON.stringify(sql)}`\n  +    : `docker exec ${DB_CONTAINER} psql -U nebu -d nebu -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -t -A -c ${JSON.stringify(sql)}`;\n  +\n  +  try {\n  +    return execSync(cmd, { stdio: 'pipe' }).toString().trim();\n  +  } catch (err) {\n  +    throw new Error(\n  +      `[fixtures/db] SQL query failed (${DB_URL ? 'NEBU_DB_URL' : `docker exec ${DB_CONTAINER}`}): ` +\n  +        `${(err as Error).message}. Is the dev stack running (make dev)?`,\n  +    );\n  +  }\n  +}\n  +\n  +/**\n  + * Upsert one server_config row (schema: 000001_init.up.sql — key PK, value\n  + * NOT NULL, set_at BIGINT NOT NULL). Used to seed per-endpoint logging\n  + * config (`log_min_level_<route>` / `log_retention_days_<route>`, the\n  + * story's Dev Notes key format) before a scenario mounts the Trace Map.\n  + */\n  +export function upsertServerConfig(key: string, value: string): void {\n  +  runSql(\n  +    `INSERT INTO server_config (key, value, set_at) VALUES (${sqlLiteral(key)}, ${sqlLiteral(value)}, ` +\n  +      'extract(epoch from now())::bigint) ' +\n  +      'ON CONFLICT (key) DO UPDATE SET value = EXCLUDED.value, set_at = EXCLUDED.set_at',\n  +  );\n  +}\n  +\n  +/** Read one server_config value ('' -> null) for scenario assertions. */\n  +export function queryServerConfigValue(key: string): string | null {\n  +  const out = runSqlQuery(`SELECT value FROM server_config WHERE key = ${sqlLiteral(key)}`);\n  +  return out === '' ? null : out;\n  +}\n  +\n  +/**\n  + * Seed one admin_temporary_captures row (expected migration:\n  + * 000020_admin_temporary_captures.up.sql — endpoint TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n  + * enabled_at/expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ, started_by TEXT). `expiresInSeconds`\n  + * may be small (about-to-expire, for the auto-revert scenarios) or large\n  + * (stable capture the scenario disables manually).\n  + */\n  +export function insertTemporaryCapture(opts: {\n  +  endpoint: string;\n  +  expiresInSeconds: number;\n  +  startedBy?: string;\n  +}): void {\n  +  const startedBy = opts.startedBy ?? {"parentUuid":"2f9d616f-17cc-4ad5-a09b-5e8b2d7fb6f2","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a7b9f01d8dbd03b21","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5","id":"msg_011CcoCuRSY6fMD7eVNhb14X","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"# Test Review Report — `trace-map-node-controls` (ATDD RED phase)\n\n**Verdict: 1 MAJOR, 4 MINOR** (plus INFO notes). The suite is otherwise well-constructed: all 13 ACs have at least one test, the UI story has 6 Playwright scenarios in `e2e/features/`, and the cluster-safe-state story has genuine restart-survival coverage.\n\n---\n\n## MAJOR\n\n**M1 — Retention options contradict the Story Amendment (retention-option mismatch).**\n`server/test/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_node_controls_test.exs:350` pins:\n\n```elixir\nassert retention_options == [\"1 h\", \"24 h\", \"7 d\", \"30 d\"]\n```\n\nThe story's Amendment (2026-07-07, ATDD flag 4 resolution) mandates the select offers **1 d / 7 d / 30 d** — \"The dummy's '1 h' option is dropped\". A correct implementation of the amended story cannot pass this test. Also stale: the moduledoc contract at line ~60 (`option labels \"1 h\" / \"24 h\" / \"7 d\" / \"30 d\"`). Fix to `[\"1 d\", \"7 d\", \"30 d\"]` and update the moduledoc. Related gap: no test pins the new \"1 d\" → `\"1\"` mapping (the \"7\"/\"30\" mappings and the \"30 d\" pre-population assertion at lines 814/872 remain valid). Feature file and steps are unaffected (Scenario A only uses \"30 d\").\n\n## MINOR\n\n**m1 — Scenario D expiry margin is flake-prone.** `e2e/features/admin/logs-trace-map-node-controls.feature:90-96` seeds a capture expiring in **4 seconds**, then navigates and asserts the countdown is visible. If login-carryover + navigation + `awaitLeitstandReady` exceed 4 s on CI, the node is already off and the intermediate assertion fails nondeterministically. Recommend 8–10 s (the revert step already waits 20 s, so nothing else changes). Scenario F's 8 s window (navigate + click + two assertions before expiry) is tighter than it needs to be too; 12–15 s costs nothing.\n\n**m2 — AC9 keyboard sub-clause unpinned.** \"Pressing Enter/Space on a focused off-node\" enabling capture (+ announcement) has no test at any level. Mitigation: the shipped hook wires Enter/Space to the same activation path as click (`trace_map.ex:785`), and leitstand E2E already covers keyboard node toggling — but the *new* off-node semantics on that path are exactly what this story changes. One keyboard-variant step in Scenario C (or a keyboard-enable LiveView test) closes it. The selects and Disable/Enable button are native elements, so their keyboard operability is fine without extra tests.\n\n**m3 — AC4 literal pin (\"not even errors\") needs a recorded PM decision, not just a handoff flag.** The pin is a faithful reading of AC4 (\"entries ... are NOT persisted\") and spec §5, and the contract test (`trace_map_node_controls_contract_test.exs:200-209`) plus the LiveView error-broadcast test pin it in exactly two places — good containment. But the operational risk is real: ERROR-level events on `/health` and `/sync` become invisible unless an operator happens to have capture enabled. The other two ATDD flags were resolved via Story Amendments; this one (handoff flag 2) is still only a flag. It should be confirmed or amended (e.g., documented errors-always-persist exception) **before** the green run, since the dev agent will otherwise cement the literal reading.\n\n**m4 — The re-enable path is never exercised.** After early disable, the LiveView test pins the \"Enable for 5 min\" button's existence and `phx-click=\"enable_capture\"` attribute, and E2E Scenario E only asserts the button is visible. No test clicks it and asserts the node returns to `temp` + a second capture row. Given the one-click amendment made this button the *only* UI for re-enabling from the inspector, one click-through assertion (Scenario E tail or the existing LiveView integration test) is cheap insurance.\n\n## INFO\n\n- **One-click enable contract (ATDD flag 1, now story-amended): consistent everywhere.** DB-free \"off-click never filters\" test, integration lifecycle tests, Scenario C, and all moduledocs/comments align; the two-step Scenario C prose is correctly superseded and documented in-file.\n- **Contract pins verified against the shipped code — real and non-overconstraining.** Migration **000020** is the correct next number (000019 is latest) and is pinned only in moduledocs, never asserted. `AuditWriter.log/6`, the `{:compliance, :repo}` FakeRepo injection, and the `@known_actions` allowlist rejection all exist (`audit_writer.ex:28,64,66`). `audit_log` column names in the integration assertions match `000001_init.up.sql` (actor_user_id/action/target_type/target_id). `data-expires-at` follows the war-room `data-started-at` precedent. `should_persist?/2` delegation assertions match the shipped `/1` clauses (error/warning → `true`, DB-free).\n- **AC12 Option B coverage is real, not vocabulary-only:** FakeRepo pins the allowlist (contract), and `temporary_capture_manager_test.exs:164-179` asserts actual `audit_log` rows naming actor and endpoint for both actions.\n- **Cascade flash regex `Updated \\d+ endpoints` is sound** — it deliberately avoids overpinning the `/admin/keepalive` cascade-membership question while the DB assertions pin the two observed satellites hard.\n- **Deliberately-green inline-JS guard: acceptable.** Documented in-file, leitstand precedent, honest about being green today; it is regression insurance for spec §8, and the ATDD handoff declares it.\n- **Determinism:** only one sleep in the whole suite (1.2 s with `temporary_capture_duration_s = 1`, blessed by answered design Q3; direction-deterministic). It pins the internal `:trace_map_tick` message name — which exists in shipped `trace_map.ex:265/307`, so it is real, though internal. All other expiry paths use seeded already-expired/about-to-expire rows; E2E countdown ticking uses `waitForFunction`/`expect.poll`, no bare sleeps.\n- **Crash/restart:** no GenServer owns capture state by design (DB row is the state); the fresh-mount-renders-active-capture and expired-row-means-off tests are the correct restart-survival pins.\n- **AC3 honesty:** the LiveView test explicitly labels itself an approximation (raw-HTML identity of the select across a stat patch, with a vacuity guard) and defers real focus to E2E Scenario A, which checks `document.activeElement` — correct division of authority.\n- **db.ts fixture:** same approved blast radius as before — `sqlLiteral` escaping, ON CONFLICT upserts, no test-only HTTP routes; `clearEndpointLoggingConfig` uses escaped-underscore LIKE patterns so the global `log_min_level` key is not clobbered. After-hooks are correctly tag-scoped.\n- **Matrix error codes / HTTP statuses: N/A** — admin LiveView only, no new HTTP endpoints; admin gating is inherited from the page gate already pinned by the leitstand suite's unauthenticated-redirect test.\n- Nit: the contract test's \"defaults to 300\" test deletes `:temporary_capture_duration_s` without restore — safe only because the key is unset in test config.\n\n## AC coverage check (all 13 covered)\n\n| AC | Covered by | Status |\n|----|-----------|--------|\n| 1, 2 | contract `cascade_targets`, LiveView markup + integration persistence/cascade/pre-population, E2E A/B | OK (AC2 option list = M1) |\n| 3 | LiveView build-once approximation + E2E A (authoritative focus) | OK |\n| 4 | contract off-list + `should_persist?/2`, LiveView rendering/propagation/signal exclusion, E2E C | OK (m3 decision pending) |\n| 5, 6, 7, 8 | integration capture lifecycle + manager test, E2E C/D/E/F | OK (m4 re-enable gap) |\n| 9 | labels/aria-labels/announcements | Partial (m2 keyboard) |\n| 10 | phx-change form + deliberately-green guard | OK |\n| 11 | 6 scenarios A–F, `@pending-feature`, bddgen-verified | OK |\n| 12 | vocabulary (contract) + real audit rows (integration) | OK |\n| 13 | `long_polling_endpoints/0` canonical constant; doc already superseded | OK |\n\n## Files reviewed\n\n- /Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/test/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_node_controls_contract_test.exs\n- /Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/test/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_node_controls_test.exs (M1 at line 350;
